<file path="cdi_optimizer/core/metadata.py" bytes="7808" lines="209" encoding="utf-8-sig">
  1 | """Read engraving dimensions and screening metadata embedded in LEN XMP blocks."""
  2 | 
  3 | import html
  4 | import math
  5 | import os
  6 | import re
  7 | from functools import lru_cache
  8 | 
  9 | _FLOAT_EXPR = rb"([0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]*)?|\.[0-9]+)(?:[eE][+-]?\d+)?"
 10 | _XMP_PATTERN = re.compile(rb"<x:xmpmeta\b.*?</x:xmpmeta>", re.DOTALL | re.IGNORECASE)
 11 | _UNITS_PATTERN = re.compile(
 12 |     rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?units\b[^>]*>\s*([a-zA-Z]+)\s*</",
 13 |     re.IGNORECASE,
 14 | )
 15 | _SIZE_PATTERNS = {
 16 |     "alto_mm": re.compile(
 17 |         rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?vsize\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</",
 18 |         re.IGNORECASE,
 19 |     ),
 20 |     "ancho_mm": re.compile(
 21 |         rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?hsize\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</",
 22 |         re.IGNORECASE,
 23 |     ),
 24 | }
 25 | _RESOLUTION_PATTERNS = {
 26 |     "resolucion_y_dpi": re.compile(
 27 |         rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?vresolution\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</",
 28 |         re.IGNORECASE,
 29 |     ),
 30 |     "resolucion_x_dpi": re.compile(
 31 |         rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?hresolution\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</",
 32 |         re.IGNORECASE,
 33 |     ),
 34 | }
 35 | _INK_NAME_PATTERNS = (
 36 |     re.compile(rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?egname\b[^>]*>\s*([^<]+?)\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 37 |     re.compile(rb"<egInk:name\b[^>]*>\s*([^<]+?)\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 38 |     re.compile(rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?inkname\b[^>]*>\s*([^<]+?)\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 39 | )
 40 | _RGB_PATTERNS = (
 41 |     re.compile(rb"<egInk:r\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 42 |     re.compile(rb"<egInk:g\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 43 |     re.compile(rb"<egInk:b\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 44 | )
 45 | # Prioriza frequencyreq y anglereq (valores nominales de preprensa) sobre los calculados por el RIP
 46 | _FREQUENCY_PATTERNS = (
 47 |     re.compile(rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?frequencyreq\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 48 |     re.compile(rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?frequency\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 49 | )
 50 | _ANGLE_PATTERNS = (
 51 |     re.compile(rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?anglereq\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 52 |     re.compile(rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?angle\b[^>]*>\s*" + _FLOAT_EXPR + rb"\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 53 | )
 54 | _DOT_PATTERNS = (
 55 |     re.compile(rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?dotname\b[^>]*>\s*([^<]+?)\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 56 |     re.compile(rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?dotshapereq\b[^>]*>\s*([^<]+?)\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 57 |     re.compile(rb"<(?:[\w.-]+:)?dotshape\b[^>]*>\s*([^<]+?)\s*</", re.IGNORECASE),
 58 | )
 59 | 
 60 | _UNIT_FACTORS_TO_MM = {
 61 |     "mm": 1.0,
 62 |     "millimeter": 1.0,
 63 |     "millimeters": 1.0,
 64 |     "cm": 10.0,
 65 |     "centimeter": 10.0,
 66 |     "centimeters": 10.0,
 67 |     "in": 25.4,
 68 |     "inch": 25.4,
 69 |     "inches": 25.4,
 70 |     "pt": 25.4 / 72.0,
 71 |     "point": 25.4 / 72.0,
 72 |     "points": 25.4 / 72.0,
 73 |     "um": 0.001,
 74 |     "micron": 0.001,
 75 |     "microns": 0.001,
 76 | }

 78 | 
 79 | def extraer_medidas_xmp(ruta_archivo):
 80 |     """Return cached dimensions and extended metadata while the source LEN file remains unchanged."""
 81 |     try:
 82 |         path = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(os.fspath(ruta_archivo)))
 83 |         stat = os.stat(path)
 84 |     except (OSError, TypeError, ValueError):
 85 |         return None
 86 |     measurements = _extraer_medidas_xmp_cache(
 87 |         path,
 88 |         stat.st_size,
 89 |         stat.st_mtime_ns,
 90 |         stat.st_ctime_ns,
 91 |     )
 92 |     return dict(measurements) if measurements is not None else None
 93 | 
 94 | 
 95 | @lru_cache(maxsize=2048)
 96 | def _extraer_medidas_xmp_cache(path, file_size, modified_ns, changed_ns):
 97 |     return _leer_medidas_xmp(path)
 98 | 
 99 | 
100 | def _extraer_texto(block, patterns):
101 |     for pattern in patterns:
102 |         match = pattern.search(block)
103 |         if match:
104 |             try:
105 |                 text = html.unescape(match.group(1).decode("utf-8", errors="replace")).strip()
106 |             except Exception:
107 |                 continue
108 |             if text:
109 |                 return text
110 |     return None
111 | 
112 | 
113 | def _extraer_flotante(block, patterns, allow_zero=False):
114 |     for pattern in patterns:
115 |         match = pattern.search(block)
116 |         if match:
117 |             try:
118 |                 value = float(match.group(0).split(b">", 1)[1].split(b"<", 1)[0].strip())
119 |             except (IndexError, ValueError):
120 |                 continue
121 |             if math.isfinite(value) and (value >= 0 if allow_zero else value > 0):
122 |                 return value
123 |     return None
124 | 
125 | 
126 | def _extraer_color_rgb(block):
127 |     channels = []
128 |     for pattern in _RGB_PATTERNS:
129 |         match = pattern.search(block)
130 |         if not match:
131 |             return None
132 |         try:
133 |             raw_text = match.group(0).split(b">", 1)[1].split(b"<", 1)[0].strip()
134 |             value = float(raw_text)
135 |         except (IndexError, ValueError):
136 |             return None
137 |         if not math.isfinite(value) or value < 0:
138 |             return None
139 |         channels.append(value)
140 |     scale = 255.0 if max(channels) <= 1.0 else 1.0
141 |     return tuple(max(0, min(255, round(channel * scale))) for channel in channels)
142 | 
143 | 
144 | def _leer_medidas_xmp(ruta_archivo):
145 |     """Return height, width in mm and extended screening info from a LEN XMP block."""
146 |     try:
147 |         with open(ruta_archivo, "rb") as file:
148 |             start = file.read(256_000)
149 |             file.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)
150 |             size = file.tell()
151 |             if size > 256_000:
152 |                 file.seek(max(0, size - 256_000))
153 |                 end = file.read(256_000)
154 |             else:
155 |                 end = b""
156 | 
157 |         blocks = [match.group(0) for match in _XMP_PATTERN.finditer(start)]
158 |         blocks.extend(match.group(0) for match in _XMP_PATTERN.finditer(end))
159 |         for block in blocks:
160 |             unit_factor = 1.0
161 |             unit_match = _UNITS_PATTERN.search(block)
162 |             if unit_match:
163 |                 unit_name = unit_match.group(1).decode("ascii", errors="ignore").strip().lower()
164 |                 unit_factor = _UNIT_FACTORS_TO_MM.get(unit_name, 1.0)
165 | 
166 |             measurements = {}
167 |             for name, pattern in _SIZE_PATTERNS.items():
168 |                 match = pattern.search(block)
169 |                 if not match:
170 |                     break
171 |                 raw_val = match.group(0).split(b">", 1)[1].split(b"<", 1)[0].strip()
172 |                 value = float(raw_val) * unit_factor
173 |                 if not math.isfinite(value) or value <= 0:
174 |                     break
175 |                 measurements[name] = round(value, 6)
176 | 
177 |             if len(measurements) == 2:
178 |                 for name, pattern in _RESOLUTION_PATTERNS.items():
179 |                     match = pattern.search(block)
180 |                     if match:
181 |                         raw_res = match.group(0).split(b">", 1)[1].split(b"<", 1)[0].strip()
182 |                         resolution = float(raw_res)
183 |                         if math.isfinite(resolution) and resolution > 0:
184 |                             measurements[name] = resolution
185 | 
186 |                 ink_name = _extraer_texto(block, _INK_NAME_PATTERNS)
187 |                 if ink_name is not None:
188 |                     measurements["tinta_nombre"] = ink_name
189 | 
190 |                 color_rgb = _extraer_color_rgb(block)
191 |                 if color_rgb is not None:
192 |                     measurements["color_rgb"] = color_rgb
193 | 
194 |                 frequency = _extraer_flotante(block, _FREQUENCY_PATTERNS, allow_zero=False)
195 |                 if frequency is not None:
196 |                     measurements["lineaje_lpi"] = round(frequency, 2)
197 | 
198 |                 angle = _extraer_flotante(block, _ANGLE_PATTERNS, allow_zero=True)
199 |                 if angle is not None:
200 |                     measurements["angulo_grados"] = round(angle, 2)
201 | 
202 |                 dot_shape = _extraer_texto(block, _DOT_PATTERNS)
203 |                 if dot_shape is not None:
204 |                     measurements["punto_trama"] = dot_shape
205 | 
206 |                 return measurements
207 |     except (OSError, ValueError):
208 |         return None
209 |     return None
</file>

<file path="cdi_optimizer/core/optimizer.py" bytes="14566" lines="416" encoding="utf-8-sig">
  1 | """Build optimized plate alternatives from selected LEN files."""
  2 | 
  3 | import logging
  4 | import os
  5 | from concurrent.futures import FIRST_COMPLETED, ProcessPoolExecutor, wait
  6 | 
  7 | from .metadata import extraer_medidas_xmp
  8 | from .packing import optimizar_piezas
  9 | from .scanner import extraer_material_fotopolimero
 10 | 
 11 | _MAX_VISIBLE_ALTERNATIVES = 10
 12 | _MAX_ALTERNATIVES_PER_BATCH = 2
 13 | _PARALLEL_PROFILE_MIN_PIECES = 16
 14 | _MAX_PARALLEL_PROFILE_WORKERS = 4
 15 | _LOGGER = logging.getLogger("cdi_optimizer")
 16 | 
 17 | _EXTENDED_META_KEYS = (
 18 |     "tinta_nombre",
 19 |     "color_rgb",
 20 |     "lineaje_lpi",
 21 |     "angulo_grados",
 22 |     "punto_trama",
 23 | )
 24 | 
 25 | 
 26 | def _variant_profiles(max_ratio, allow_rotation):
 27 |     return (
 28 |         ("sin_giro_individual", min(1.5, max_ratio), 50, True, False),
 29 |         ("ratio_compacto", min(1.25, max_ratio), 30, False, allow_rotation),
 30 |         ("ratio_equilibrado", min(1.75, max_ratio), 55, True, allow_rotation),
 31 |         ("ratio_alargado", min(2.25, max_ratio), 75, False, allow_rotation),
 32 |         ("ratio_alargado_agrupado", min(2.5, max_ratio), 70, True, allow_rotation),
 33 |     )
 34 | 
 35 | 
 36 | def _cancelled(cancel_event):
 37 |     return cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set()
 38 | 
 39 | 
 40 | def _optimizar_perfil_en_proceso(arguments):
 41 |     pieces, profile_config = arguments
 42 |     return optimizar_piezas(pieces, profile_config)
 43 | 
 44 | 
 45 | def _evaluar_perfiles_secuencialmente(
 46 |     pieces, profile_configs, cancel_event, progress_callback=None
 47 | ):
 48 |     results = []
 49 |     for index, profile_config in enumerate(profile_configs, start=1):
 50 |         if _cancelled(cancel_event):
 51 |             return None
 52 |         results.append(
 53 |             optimizar_piezas(pieces, profile_config, cancel_event=cancel_event)
 54 |         )
 55 |         if progress_callback:
 56 |             percent = 20 + round(index / len(profile_configs) * 75)
 57 |             progress_callback(percent, f"Perfil {index}/{len(profile_configs)} completado")
 58 |     return results
 59 | 
 60 | 
 61 | def _evaluar_perfiles(
 62 |     pieces, profile_configs, cancel_event=None, progress_callback=None
 63 | ):
 64 |     if _cancelled(cancel_event):
 65 |         return None
 66 |     if (
 67 |         len(pieces) < _PARALLEL_PROFILE_MIN_PIECES
 68 |         or len(profile_configs) < 2
 69 |         or (os.cpu_count() or 1) < 2
 70 |     ):
 71 |         return _evaluar_perfiles_secuencialmente(
 72 |             pieces, profile_configs, cancel_event, progress_callback
 73 |         )
 74 |     worker_count = min(
 75 |         _MAX_PARALLEL_PROFILE_WORKERS,
 76 |         len(profile_configs),
 77 |         os.cpu_count() or 1,
 78 |     )
 79 |     results = [None] * len(profile_configs)
 80 |     try:
 81 |         with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=worker_count) as executor:
 82 |             futures = {
 83 |                 executor.submit(
 84 |                     _optimizar_perfil_en_proceso, (pieces, profile_config)
 85 |                 ): index
 86 |                 for index, profile_config in enumerate(profile_configs)
 87 |             }
 88 |             pending = set(futures)
 89 |             completed_count = 0
 90 |             while pending:
 91 |                 if _cancelled(cancel_event):
 92 |                     for future in pending:
 93 |                         future.cancel()
 94 |                     return None
 95 |                 completed, pending = wait(
 96 |                     pending,
 97 |                     timeout=0.1,
 98 |                     return_when=FIRST_COMPLETED,
 99 |                 )
100 |                 for future in completed:
101 |                     results[futures[future]] = future.result()
102 |                     completed_count += 1
103 |                     if progress_callback:
104 |                         percent = 20 + round(
105 |                             completed_count / len(profile_configs) * 75
106 |                         )
107 |                         progress_callback(
108 |                             percent,
109 |                             f"Perfiles completados: {completed_count}/{len(profile_configs)}",
110 |                         )
111 |     except Exception:
112 |         if _cancelled(cancel_event):
113 |             return None
114 |         _LOGGER.exception(
115 |             "No se pudieron evaluar perfiles en paralelo; se reintenta en serie"
116 |         )
117 |         return _evaluar_perfiles_secuencialmente(
118 |             pieces, profile_configs, cancel_event, progress_callback
119 |         )
120 |     return results
121 | 
122 | 
123 | def _leer_piezas(lista_archivos, cancel_event=None, progress_callback=None):
124 |     piezas = []
125 |     errores = []
126 |     if _cancelled(cancel_event):
127 |         return piezas, errores, True
128 |     total = len(lista_archivos)
129 |     for index, ruta in enumerate(lista_archivos, start=1):
130 |         if _cancelled(cancel_event):
131 |             return piezas, errores, True
132 |         medidas = extraer_medidas_xmp(ruta)
133 |         if medidas is None:
134 |             errores.append(
135 |                 f"No se pudieron leer las dimensiones de {os.path.basename(ruta)}."
136 |             )
137 |         else:
138 |             pieza = {
139 |                 "name": os.path.basename(ruta),
140 |                 "path": ruta,
141 |                 "width_mm": medidas["ancho_mm"],
142 |                 "height_mm": medidas["alto_mm"],
143 |                 "material_fotopolimero": extraer_material_fotopolimero(ruta),
144 |                 "resolucion_dpi": (
145 |                     medidas.get("resolucion_x_dpi"),
146 |                     medidas.get("resolucion_y_dpi"),
147 |                 )
148 |                 if "resolucion_x_dpi" in medidas
149 |                 and "resolucion_y_dpi" in medidas
150 |                 else None,
151 |             }
152 |             for extra_key in _EXTENDED_META_KEYS:
153 |                 if extra_key in medidas:
154 |                     pieza[extra_key] = medidas[extra_key]
155 |             piezas.append(pieza)
156 |         if progress_callback:
157 |             progress_callback(
158 |                 round(index / max(1, total) * 20),
159 |                 f"Leyendo metadatos: {index}/{total} archivos",
160 |             )
161 |     return piezas, errores, False
162 | 
163 | 
164 | def _validar_resoluciones(piezas):
165 |     resolutions = {piece["resolucion_dpi"] for piece in piezas}
166 |     return len(piezas) <= 1 or (None not in resolutions and len(resolutions) <= 1)
167 | 
168 | 
169 | def _validar_materiales_fotopolimero(piezas):
170 |     materials = {piece.get("material_fotopolimero") for piece in piezas}
171 |     return len(materials) <= 1
172 | 
173 | 
174 | def _error_resolucion():
175 |     return [
176 |         "No se pueden combinar trabajos con distintas resoluciones DPI "
177 |         "o sin resolución verificable."
178 |     ]
179 | 
180 | 
181 | def _error_material_fotopolimero():
182 |     return [
183 |         "No se pueden combinar trabajos con tipos de fotopolímero distintos "
184 |         "o con archivos sin tipo verificable."
185 |     ]
186 | 
187 | 
188 | def optimizar_archivos(lista_archivos, config=None, cancel_event=None):
189 |     piezas, errores, cancelled = _leer_piezas(lista_archivos, cancel_event)
190 |     if cancelled:
191 |         return {
192 |             "placa_unica": None,
193 |             "placas_dobles": None,
194 |             "errores": [],
195 |             "cancelado": True,
196 |         }
197 |     if not _validar_resoluciones(piezas):
198 |         return {
199 |             "placa_unica": None,
200 |             "placas_dobles": None,
201 |             "errores": _error_resolucion(),
202 |         }
203 |     if not _validar_materiales_fotopolimero(piezas):
204 |         return {
205 |             "placa_unica": None,
206 |             "placas_dobles": None,
207 |             "errores": _error_material_fotopolimero(),
208 |         }
209 |     resultado = optimizar_piezas(piezas, config, cancel_event=cancel_event)
210 |     resultado["errores"] = errores + resultado["errores"]
211 |     return resultado
212 | 
213 | 
214 | def _geometry_signature(solution):
215 |     placements = tuple(
216 |         sorted(
217 |             (
218 |                 round(part["x"], 1),
219 |                 round(part["y"], 1),
220 |                 round(part["w"], 1),
221 |                 round(part["h"], 1),
222 |                 bool(part.get("rotado")),
223 |                 part.get("placa", 1),
224 |             )
225 |             for part in solution["grabados"]
226 |         )
227 |     )
228 |     panels = tuple(
229 |         (round(panel["ancho"], 1), round(panel["alto"], 1))
230 |         for panel in solution.get("placas", [])
231 |     )
232 |     return (
233 |         solution["tipo"],
234 |         round(solution["ancho"], 1),
235 |         round(solution["alto"], 1),
236 |         panels,
237 |         placements,
238 |     )
239 | 
240 | 
241 | def generar_alternativas_archivos(
242 |     lista_archivos,
243 |     config=None,
244 |     cancel_event=None,
245 |     *,
246 |     variant_batch=None,
247 |     existing_alternatives=(),
248 |     progress_callback=None,
249 | ):
250 |     existing_alternatives = tuple(existing_alternatives)
251 |     if _cancelled(cancel_event):
252 |         return {"alternativas": [], "errores": [], "cancelado": True}
253 |     if variant_batch is not None and (
254 |         variant_batch < 0
255 |         or len(existing_alternatives) >= _MAX_VISIBLE_ALTERNATIVES
256 |     ):
257 |         return {
258 |             "alternativas": [],
259 |             "errores": [],
260 |             "cancelado": False,
261 |             "hay_mas_variantes": False,
262 |         }
263 | 
264 |     pieces, errors, cancelled = _leer_piezas(
265 |         lista_archivos, cancel_event, progress_callback
266 |     )
267 |     if cancelled:
268 |         return {"alternativas": [], "errores": [], "cancelado": True}
269 |     if not _validar_resoluciones(pieces):
270 |         return {"alternativas": [], "errores": _error_resolucion(), "cancelado": False}
271 |     if not _validar_materiales_fotopolimero(pieces):
272 |         return {
273 |             "alternativas": [],
274 |             "errores": _error_material_fotopolimero(),
275 |             "cancelado": False,
276 |         }
277 |     if not pieces:
278 |         return {"alternativas": [], "errores": errors, "cancelado": False}
279 | 
280 |     base_config = dict(config or {})
281 |     max_ratio = min(10.0, max(1.0, float(base_config.get("ratio_maximo", 3.0))))
282 |     allow_rotation = bool(base_config.get("rotacion", True))
283 |     balanced_ratio = min(1.5, max_ratio)
284 |     initial_profiles = (
285 |         ("maximum_use", 1.0, 0, False),
286 |         ("balanced", balanced_ratio, 50, False),
287 |         ("drum_safe", max_ratio, 100, False),
288 |         ("dimension_grouped", balanced_ratio, 50, True),
289 |     )
290 |     variant_profiles = _variant_profiles(max_ratio, allow_rotation)
291 | 
292 |     if variant_batch is None:
293 |         profiles = tuple(
294 |             (*profile, allow_rotation) for profile in initial_profiles
295 |         )
296 |         known = set()
297 |     else:
298 |         if (
299 |             variant_batch < 0
300 |             or variant_batch >= len(variant_profiles)
301 |             or len(existing_alternatives) >= _MAX_VISIBLE_ALTERNATIVES
302 |         ):
303 |             return {
304 |                 "alternativas": [],
305 |                 "errores": errors,
306 |                 "cancelado": False,
307 |                 "hay_mas_variantes": False,
308 |             }
309 |         profiles = (variant_profiles[variant_batch],)
310 |         known = {
311 |             _geometry_signature(solution) for solution in existing_alternatives
312 |         }
313 | 
314 |     profile_configs = [
315 |         {
316 |             **base_config,
317 |             "prioridad_aspecto": area_tolerance,
318 |             "ratio_objetivo": target_ratio,
319 |             "agrupar_dimensiones": group_dimensions,
320 |             "rotacion": profile_rotation,
321 |             "ratio_maximo": max_ratio,
322 |         }
323 |         for (
324 |             _,
325 |             target_ratio,
326 |             area_tolerance,
327 |             group_dimensions,
328 |             profile_rotation,
329 |         ) in profiles
330 |     ]
331 |     profile_results = _evaluar_perfiles(
332 |         pieces, profile_configs, cancel_event, progress_callback
333 |     )
334 |     if profile_results is None or _cancelled(cancel_event):
335 |         return {"alternativas": [], "errores": [], "cancelado": True}
336 | 
337 |     alternatives = []
338 |     seen = set(known)
339 |     ratio_rejected = False
340 |     for profile_index, (profile, result) in enumerate(zip(profiles, profile_results)):
341 |         if _cancelled(cancel_event):
342 |             return {"alternativas": [], "errores": [], "cancelado": True}
343 |         if result.get("cancelado") or _cancelled(cancel_event):
344 |             return {"alternativas": [], "errores": [], "cancelado": True}
345 |         profile_name = profile[0]
346 |         for key, kind in (("placa_unica", "single"), ("placas_dobles", "split")):
347 |             board = result.get(key)
348 |             if board is None:
349 |                 continue
350 |             if board.get("ratio_fuera_limite", False):
351 |                 ratio_rejected = True
352 |                 continue
353 |             solution = {
354 |                 **board,
355 |                 "tipo": kind,
356 |                 "perfil_interno": profile_name,
357 |                 "_perfil_indice": profile_index,
358 |                 "ratio_alto_ancho": board["alto"] / board["ancho"],
359 |             }
360 |             signature = _geometry_signature(solution)
361 |             if signature not in seen:
362 |                 seen.add(signature)
363 |                 alternatives.append(solution)
364 | 
365 |     def option_rank(option):
366 |         return (
367 |             -option["eficiencia_exacta"],
368 |             option["_perfil_indice"],
369 |             abs(option["ratio_alto_ancho"] - 1.5),
370 |         )
371 | 
372 |     if variant_batch is None:
373 |         singles = [option for option in alternatives if option["tipo"] == "single"]
374 |         splits = [option for option in alternatives if option["tipo"] == "split"]
375 |         selected = []
376 |         if singles:
377 |             selected.append(min(singles, key=option_rank))
378 |         if splits:
379 |             selected.append(min(splits, key=option_rank))
380 |         selected_signatures = {_geometry_signature(option) for option in selected}
381 |         for option in sorted(alternatives, key=option_rank):
382 |             signature = _geometry_signature(option)
383 |             if signature in selected_signatures:
384 |                 continue
385 |             selected.append(option)
386 |             selected_signatures.add(signature)
387 |             if len(selected) == 4:
388 |                 break
389 |         alternatives = selected
390 |         more_variants = bool(alternatives)
391 |     else:
392 |         available_slots = _MAX_VISIBLE_ALTERNATIVES - len(existing_alternatives)
393 |         alternatives = sorted(alternatives, key=option_rank)[
394 |             : min(_MAX_ALTERNATIVES_PER_BATCH, available_slots)
395 |         ]
396 |         more_variants = (
397 |             variant_batch + 1 < len(variant_profiles)
398 |             and len(existing_alternatives) + len(alternatives)
399 |             < _MAX_VISIBLE_ALTERNATIVES
400 |         )
401 | 
402 |     for option in alternatives:
403 |         option.pop("_perfil_indice", None)
404 | 
405 |     if not alternatives and ratio_rejected:
406 |         errors.append(
407 |             "Los grabados no caben en el ratio máximo configurado. "
408 |             "Aumenta el límite en Preferencias para esta selección."
409 |         )
410 | 
411 |     return {
412 |         "alternativas": alternatives,
413 |         "errores": errors,
414 |         "cancelado": _cancelled(cancel_event),
415 |         "hay_mas_variantes": more_variants,
416 |     }
</file>

<file path="cdi_optimizer/core/packing.py" bytes="36198" lines="1002" encoding="utf-8-sig">
   1 | """Heuristic rectangle packing for CDI plate layouts."""

   9 |     "color_rgb",

  16 | def _extra_item_metadata(item):
  17 |     return {key: item[key] for key in _METADATA_KEYS if key in item and item[key] is not None}

  19 | 
  20 | def _intersects(first, second):
  21 |     return not (
  22 |         first[0] >= second[0] + second[2]
  23 |         or first[0] + first[2] <= second[0]
  24 |         or first[1] >= second[1] + second[3]
  25 |         or first[1] + first[3] <= second[1]
  26 |     )
  27 | 
  28 | 
  29 | def _contains(first, second):
  30 |     return (
  31 |         first[0] <= second[0]
  32 |         and first[1] <= second[1]
  33 |         and first[0] + first[2] >= second[0] + second[2]
  34 |         and first[1] + first[3] >= second[1] + second[3]
  35 |     )
  36 | 
  37 | 
  38 | def _overlaps_parts(first, second, spacing=0):
  39 |     return (
  40 |         first["x"] < second["x"] + second["w"] + spacing
  41 |         and first["x"] + first["w"] > second["x"] - spacing
  42 |         and first["y"] < second["y"] + second["h"] + spacing
  43 |         and first["y"] + first["h"] > second["y"] - spacing
  44 |     )
  45 | 
  46 | 
  47 | def _compact_bottom_left(parts, width, height, spacing):
  48 |     """Slide placements down and left without changing their dimensions."""
  49 |     compacted = [dict(part) for part in parts]
  50 |     for _ in range(max(1, len(compacted) * 2)):
  51 |         changed = False
  52 |         for part in sorted(compacted, key=lambda item: (-item["y"], item["x"])):
  53 |             px, pw = part["x"], part["w"]
  54 |             # Solo las piezas que se solapan horizontalmente pueden colisionar al deslizar en Y
  55 |             x_neighbors = [
  56 |                 other
  57 |                 for other in compacted
  58 |                 if other is not part
  59 |                 and px < other["x"] + other["w"] + spacing
  60 |                 and px + pw > other["x"] - spacing
  61 |             ]
  62 |             y_candidates = {part["y"], height - part["h"]}
  63 |             for other in x_neighbors:
  64 |                 if px < other["x"] + other["w"] and px + pw > other["x"]:
  65 |                     y_candidates.update(
  66 |                         (
  67 |                             other["y"] - part["h"] - spacing,
  68 |                             other["y"] + other["h"] + spacing,
  69 |                         )
  70 |                     )
  71 |             valid_y = []
  72 |             for candidate_y in y_candidates:
  73 |                 if (
  74 |                     candidate_y < part["y"] - 1e-7
  75 |                     or candidate_y < 0
  76 |                     or candidate_y + part["h"] > height + 1e-7
  77 |                 ):
  78 |                     continue
  79 |                 candidate = {**part, "y": candidate_y}
  80 |                 if not any(_overlaps_parts(candidate, other, spacing) for other in x_neighbors):
  81 |                     valid_y.append(candidate_y)
  82 |             if valid_y:
  83 |                 new_y = max(valid_y)
  84 |                 changed = changed or new_y > part["y"] + 1e-7
  85 |                 part["y"] = new_y
  86 | 
  87 |         for part in sorted(compacted, key=lambda item: (item["y"], item["x"])):
  88 |             py, ph = part["y"], part["h"]
  89 |             # Solo las piezas que se solapan verticalmente pueden colisionar al deslizar en X
  90 |             y_neighbors = [
  91 |                 other
  92 |                 for other in compacted
  93 |                 if other is not part
  94 |                 and py < other["y"] + other["h"] + spacing
  95 |                 and py + ph > other["y"] - spacing
  96 |             ]
  97 |             x_candidates = {part["x"], 0.0}
  98 |             for other in y_neighbors:
  99 |                 if py < other["y"] + other["h"] and py + ph > other["y"]:
 100 |                     x_candidates.update(
 101 |                         (
 102 |                             other["x"] - part["w"] - spacing,
 103 |                             other["x"] + other["w"] + spacing,
 104 |                         )
 105 |                     )
 106 |             valid_x = []
 107 |             for candidate_x in x_candidates:
 108 |                 if (
 109 |                     candidate_x > part["x"] + 1e-7
 110 |                     or candidate_x < 0
 111 |                     or candidate_x + part["w"] > width + 1e-7
 112 |                 ):
 113 |                     continue
 114 |                 candidate = {**part, "x": candidate_x}
 115 |                 if not any(_overlaps_parts(candidate, other, spacing) for other in y_neighbors):
 116 |                     valid_x.append(candidate_x)
 117 |             if valid_x:
 118 |                 new_x = min(valid_x)
 119 |                 changed = changed or new_x < part["x"] - 1e-7
 120 |                 part["x"] = new_x
 121 |         if not changed:
 122 |             break
 123 |     return compacted

 125 | 
 126 | def _split_free_rectangles(free_rectangles, used):
 127 |     split_rectangles = []
 128 |     for free in free_rectangles:
 129 |         if not _intersects(free, used):
 130 |             split_rectangles.append(free)
 131 |             continue
 132 |         free_x, free_y, free_w, free_h = free
 133 |         used_x, used_y, used_w, used_h = used
 134 |         if used_x < free_x + free_w and used_x + used_w > free_x:
 135 |             if used_y > free_y and used_y < free_y + free_h:
 136 |                 split_rectangles.append((free_x, free_y, free_w, used_y - free_y))
 137 |             if used_y + used_h < free_y + free_h:
 138 |                 split_rectangles.append(
 139 |                     (free_x, used_y + used_h, free_w, free_y + free_h - used_y - used_h)
 140 |                 )
 141 |         if used_y < free_y + free_h and used_y + used_h > free_y:
 142 |             if used_x > free_x and used_x < free_x + free_w:
 143 |                 split_rectangles.append((free_x, free_y, used_x - free_x, free_h))
 144 |             if used_x + used_w < free_x + free_w:
 145 |                 split_rectangles.append(
 146 |                     (used_x + used_w, free_y, free_x + free_w - used_x - used_w, free_h)
 147 |                 )
 148 | 
 149 |     pruned = []
 150 |     for index, rectangle in enumerate(split_rectangles):
 151 |         if rectangle[2] <= 0 or rectangle[3] <= 0:
 152 |             continue
 153 |         if any(
 154 |             index != other_index and _contains(other, rectangle)
 155 |             for other_index, other in enumerate(split_rectangles)
 156 |         ):
 157 |             continue
 158 |         pruned.append(rectangle)
 159 |     return pruned
 160 | 
 161 | 
 162 | def _sort_items(items, order):
 163 |     if order == "given":
 164 |         return list(items)
 165 |     keys = {
 166 |         "area": lambda item: item["width_mm"] * item["height_mm"],
 167 |         "long_side": lambda item: max(item["width_mm"], item["height_mm"]),
 168 |         "short_side": lambda item: min(item["width_mm"], item["height_mm"]),
 169 |         "width": lambda item: item["width_mm"],
 170 |         "height": lambda item: item["height_mm"],
 171 |     }
 172 |     keys["dimensions"] = lambda item: (
 173 |         round(item["width_mm"], 3),
 174 |         round(item["height_mm"], 3),
 175 |     )
 176 |     return sorted(items, key=lambda item: keys[order](item), reverse=True)
 177 | 
 178 | 
 179 | def _pack_order(
 180 |     items,
 181 |     bin_width,
 182 |     bin_height,
 183 |     spacing,
 184 |     allow_rotation,
 185 |     order,
 186 |     target_ratio=1.0,
 187 |     cancel_event=None,
 188 |     priority=0,
 189 | ):
 190 |     free_rectangles = [(0.0, 0.0, bin_width, bin_height)]
 191 |     placed = []
 192 |     used_width = 0.0
 193 |     used_height = 0.0
 194 |     aspect_weight = 0.03 * (priority / 100.0) if priority > 0 else 0.0
 195 | 
 196 |     for item in _sort_items(items, order):
 197 |         if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
 198 |             return None
 199 |         width = float(item["width_mm"]) + 2 * spacing
 200 |         height = float(item["height_mm"]) + 2 * spacing
 201 |         candidates = [(width, height, False)]
 202 |         if allow_rotation and width != height:
 203 |             candidates.append((height, width, True))
 204 |         best = None
 205 |         for free_index, free in enumerate(free_rectangles):
 206 |             for candidate_width, candidate_height, rotated in candidates:
 207 |                 leftover_width = free[2] - candidate_width
 208 |                 leftover_height = free[3] - candidate_height
 209 |                 if leftover_width < -1e-7 or leftover_height < -1e-7:
 210 |                     continue
 211 |                 bounds_width = max(used_width, free[0] + candidate_width)
 212 |                 bounds_height = max(used_height, free[1] + candidate_height)
 213 |                 aspect_ratio = bounds_height / bounds_width
 214 |                 ratio_distance = abs(math.log(aspect_ratio / target_ratio))
 215 |                 envelope_cost = (bounds_width * bounds_height) * (
 216 |                     1.0 + aspect_weight * min(1.0, ratio_distance)
 217 |                 )
 218 |                 score = (
 219 |                     envelope_cost,
 220 |                     ratio_distance,
 221 |                     min(leftover_width, leftover_height),
 222 |                     max(leftover_width, leftover_height),
 223 |                     free[1],
 224 |                     free[0],
 225 |                     int(rotated),
 226 |                 )
 227 |                 if best is None or score < best[0]:
 228 |                     best = (
 229 |                         score,
 230 |                         free_index,
 231 |                         candidate_width,
 232 |                         candidate_height,
 233 |                         rotated,
 234 |                         free,
 235 |                     )
 236 |         if best is None:
 237 |             return None
 238 |         _, _, placed_width, placed_height, rotated, free = best
 239 |         used = (free[0], free[1], placed_width, placed_height)
 240 |         free_rectangles = _split_free_rectangles(free_rectangles, used)
 241 |         actual_width = float(item["height_mm"] if rotated else item["width_mm"])
 242 |         actual_height = float(item["width_mm"] if rotated else item["height_mm"])
 243 |         used_width = max(used_width, used[0] + placed_width)
 244 |         used_height = max(used_height, used[1] + placed_height)
 245 |         placed.append(
 246 |             {
 247 |                 "nombre": item.get("name", "Grabado"),
 248 |                 "ruta": item.get("path"),
 249 |                 "x": used[0],
 250 |                 "y": used[1],
 251 |                 "w": actual_width + 2 * spacing,
 252 |                 "h": actual_height + 2 * spacing,
 253 |                 "rotado": rotated,
 254 |                 **_extra_item_metadata(item),
 255 |             }
 256 |         )
 257 | 
 258 |     used_width = max(part["x"] + part["w"] for part in placed)
 259 |     used_height = max(part["y"] + part["h"] for part in placed)
 260 |     placed = _compact_bottom_left(placed, used_width, used_height, 0)
 261 |     used_width = max(part["x"] + part["w"] for part in placed)
 262 |     used_height = max(part["y"] + part["h"] for part in placed)
 263 |     actual_parts = [
 264 |         {
 265 |             **part,
 266 |             "x": part["x"] + spacing,
 267 |             "y": part["y"] + spacing,
 268 |             "w": part["w"] - 2 * spacing,
 269 |             "h": part["h"] - 2 * spacing,
 270 |         }
 271 |         for part in placed
 272 |     ]
 273 |     return {
 274 |         "grabados": actual_parts,
 275 |         "ancho_util": used_width,
 276 |         "alto_util": used_height,
 277 |     }

 279 | 
 280 | def _area_without_spacing(packed):
 281 |     cached = packed.get("_area_sin_espaciado")
 282 |     if cached is not None:
 283 |         return cached
 284 |     parts = packed["grabados"]
 285 |     if not parts:
 286 |         packed["_area_sin_espaciado"] = 0
 287 |         return 0
 288 |     compacted = _compact_bottom_left(
 289 |         parts, packed["ancho_util"], packed["alto_util"], 0
 290 |     )
 291 |     left = min(part["x"] for part in compacted)
 292 |     top = min(part["y"] for part in compacted)
 293 |     right = max(part["x"] + part["w"] for part in compacted)
 294 |     bottom = max(part["y"] + part["h"] for part in compacted)
 295 |     area = (right - left) * (bottom - top)
 296 |     packed["_area_sin_espaciado"] = area
 297 |     return area

 299 | 
 300 | def _identical_grid_candidates(
 301 |     items, max_width, max_height, spacing, allow_rotation, cancel_event=None
 302 | ):
 303 |     if not items:
 304 |         return []
 305 |     first = items[0]
 306 |     if any(
 307 |         abs(item["width_mm"] - first["width_mm"]) > 1e-3
 308 |         or abs(item["height_mm"] - first["height_mm"]) > 1e-3
 309 |         for item in items[1:]
 310 |     ):
 311 |         return []
 312 |     orientations = [(first["width_mm"], first["height_mm"], False)]
 313 |     if allow_rotation and abs(first["width_mm"] - first["height_mm"]) > 1e-9:
 314 |         orientations.append((first["height_mm"], first["width_mm"], True))
 315 |     candidates = []
 316 |     for width, height, rotated in orientations:
 317 |         padded_width = width + 2 * spacing
 318 |         padded_height = height + 2 * spacing
 319 |         for columns in range(1, len(items) + 1):
 320 |             if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
 321 |                 return candidates
 322 |             rows = math.ceil(len(items) / columns)
 323 |             used_width = columns * padded_width
 324 |             used_height = rows * padded_height
 325 |             if used_width > max_width + 1e-7 or used_height > max_height + 1e-7:
 326 |                 continue
 327 |             placed = []
 328 |             for index, item in enumerate(items):
 329 |                 if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
 330 |                     return candidates
 331 |                 column = index % columns
 332 |                 row = index // columns
 333 |                 placed.append(
 334 |                     {
 335 |                         "nombre": item.get("name", "Grabado"),
 336 |                         "ruta": item.get("path"),
 337 |                         "x": column * padded_width + spacing,
 338 |                         "y": row * padded_height + spacing,
 339 |                         "w": width,
 340 |                         "h": height,
 341 |                         "rotado": rotated,
 342 |                         **_extra_item_metadata(item),
 343 |                     }
 344 |                 )
 345 |             candidates.append(
 346 |                 {
 347 |                     "grabados": placed,
 348 |                     "ancho_util": used_width,
 349 |                     "alto_util": used_height,
 350 |                 }
 351 |             )
 352 |     return candidates
 353 | 
 354 | 
 355 | def _transpose_packed(packed, spacing=0):
 356 |     if packed is None:
 357 |         return None
 358 |     width = packed["alto_util"]
 359 |     height = packed["ancho_util"]
 360 |     transposed = [
 361 |         {
 362 |             **part,
 363 |             "x": width - part["y"] - part["h"],
 364 |             "y": part["x"],
 365 |             "w": part["h"],
 366 |             "h": part["w"],
 367 |             "rotado": not part["rotado"],
 368 |         }
 369 |         for part in packed["grabados"]
 370 |     ]
 371 |     padded = [
 372 |         {
 373 |             **part,
 374 |             "x": part["x"] - spacing,
 375 |             "y": part["y"] - spacing,
 376 |             "w": part["w"] + 2 * spacing,
 377 |             "h": part["h"] + 2 * spacing,
 378 |         }
 379 |         for part in transposed
 380 |     ]
 381 |     compacted = _compact_bottom_left(padded, width, height, 0)
 382 |     return {
 383 |         "grabados": [
 384 |             {
 385 |                 **part,
 386 |                 "x": part["x"] + spacing,
 387 |                 "y": part["y"] + spacing,
 388 |                 "w": part["w"] - 2 * spacing,
 389 |                 "h": part["h"] - 2 * spacing,
 390 |             }
 391 |             for part in compacted
 392 |         ],
 393 |         "ancho_util": width,
 394 |         "alto_util": height,
 395 |     }
 396 | 
 397 | 
 398 | def _candidate_widths(items, max_width, spacing, allow_rotation):
 399 |     lower_bound = (
 400 |         max(
 401 |             min(item["width_mm"], item["height_mm"])
 402 |             if allow_rotation
 403 |             else item["width_mm"]
 404 |             for item in items
 405 |         )
 406 |         + 2 * spacing
 407 |     )
 408 |     if lower_bound > max_width:
 409 |         return []
 410 |     candidates = {float(max_width), float(lower_bound)}
 411 |     for fraction in (0.25, 0.4, 0.55, 0.7, 0.85):
 412 |         candidates.add(lower_bound + (max_width - lower_bound) * fraction)
 413 |     candidates.update(
 414 |         min(max_width, float(item["width_mm"]) + 2 * spacing) for item in items
 415 |     )
 416 |     repeated_widths = {}
 417 |     for item in items:
 418 |         orientations = [(item["width_mm"], item["height_mm"])]
 419 |         if allow_rotation and item["width_mm"] != item["height_mm"]:
 420 |             orientations.append((item["height_mm"], item["width_mm"]))
 421 |         for width, _ in orientations:
 422 |             padded_width = width + 2 * spacing
 423 |             repeated_widths[padded_width] = repeated_widths.get(padded_width, 0) + 1
 424 |     for width, repetitions in repeated_widths.items():
 425 |         for columns in range(2, min(repetitions, len(items)) + 1):
 426 |             candidates.add(width * columns)
 427 |     return sorted(width for width in candidates if lower_bound <= width <= max_width)
 428 | 
 429 | 
 430 | def _best_pack(
 431 |     items,
 432 |     max_width,
 433 |     max_height,
 434 |     spacing,
 435 |     allow_rotation,
 436 |     width_search=True,
 437 |     priority=0,
 438 |     target_ratio=1.0,
 439 |     group_dimensions=False,
 440 |     cancel_event=None,
 441 |     max_ratio=10.0,
 442 | ):
 443 |     if not items:
 444 |         return None
 445 |     widths = (
 446 |         _candidate_widths(items, max_width, spacing, allow_rotation)
 447 |         if width_search
 448 |         else [max_width]
 449 |     )
 450 |     if not widths:
 451 |         return None
 452 |     candidates = []
 453 |     orderings = ("area", "long_side", "short_side", "width", "height")
 454 |     if group_dimensions:
 455 |         orderings += ("dimensions",)
 456 |     priority_passes = (0, priority) if priority > 0 else (0,)
 457 |     for width in widths:
 458 |         if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
 459 |             return None
 460 |         for order in orderings:
 461 |             for pass_priority in priority_passes:
 462 |                 packed = _pack_order(
 463 |                     items,
 464 |                     width,
 465 |                     max_height,
 466 |                     spacing,
 467 |                     allow_rotation,
 468 |                     order,
 469 |                     target_ratio,
 470 |                     cancel_event,
 471 |                     priority=pass_priority,
 472 |                 )
 473 |                 if packed is None:
 474 |                     continue
 475 |                 area = packed["ancho_util"] * packed["alto_util"]
 476 |                 aspect_ratio = packed["alto_util"] / packed["ancho_util"]
 477 |                 candidates.append(
 478 |                     (area, abs(math.log(aspect_ratio / target_ratio)), packed)
 479 |                 )
 480 |     for packed in _identical_grid_candidates(
 481 |         items, max_width, max_height, spacing, allow_rotation, cancel_event
 482 |     ):
 483 |         area = packed["ancho_util"] * packed["alto_util"]
 484 |         aspect_ratio = packed["alto_util"] / packed["ancho_util"]
 485 |         candidates.append(
 486 |             (area, abs(math.log(aspect_ratio / target_ratio)), packed)
 487 |         )
 488 |     if not candidates:
 489 |         return None
 490 |     ratio_safe_candidates = [
 491 |         candidate
 492 |         for candidate in candidates
 493 |         if max(
 494 |             candidate[2]["alto_util"] / candidate[2]["ancho_util"],
 495 |             candidate[2]["ancho_util"] / candidate[2]["alto_util"],
 496 |         )
 497 |         <= max_ratio + 1e-9
 498 |     ]
 499 |     if ratio_safe_candidates:
 500 |         candidates = ratio_safe_candidates
 501 |     minimum_area = min(candidate[0] for candidate in candidates)
 502 |     area_tolerance = 0.03 * priority / 100
 503 |     eligible = [
 504 |         candidate
 505 |         for candidate in candidates
 506 |         if candidate[0] <= minimum_area * (1 + area_tolerance) + 1e-9
 507 |     ]
 508 |     best = min(eligible, key=lambda candidate: (candidate[1], candidate[0]))[2]
 509 |     if allow_rotation and any(
 510 |         item["width_mm"] != item["height_mm"] for item in items
 511 |     ):
 512 |         baseline = _best_pack(
 513 |             items,
 514 |             max_width,
 515 |             max_height,
 516 |             spacing,
 517 |             False,
 518 |             width_search,
 519 |             priority,
 520 |             target_ratio,
 521 |             group_dimensions,
 522 |             cancel_event,
 523 |             max_ratio,
 524 |         )
 525 |         if (
 526 |             baseline
 527 |             and _area_without_spacing(best) > _area_without_spacing(baseline) + 1e-9
 528 |         ):
 529 |             return baseline
 530 |     return best
 531 | 
 532 | 
 533 | def _as_board(packed, config):
 534 |     left = config["margen_izquierdo"]
 535 |     top = config["margen_superior"]
 536 |     right = config["margen_derecho"]
 537 |     clamp = config["tolerancia"]
 538 |     grip = config["pinza"]
 539 |     positioned = [
 540 |         {**part, "x": round(part["x"] + left, 2), "y": round(part["y"] + top, 2)}
 541 |         for part in packed["grabados"]
 542 |     ]
 543 |     width = round(packed["ancho_util"] + left + right + clamp, 2)
 544 |     height = round(packed["alto_util"] + top + grip + clamp, 2)
 545 |     item_area = sum(part["w"] * part["h"] for part in positioned)
 546 |     usable_area = max(0, packed["ancho_util"]) * max(0, packed["alto_util"])
 547 |     efficiency_area = _area_without_spacing(packed)
 548 |     efficiency = item_area / efficiency_area * 100 if efficiency_area else 0
 549 |     return {
 550 |         "ancho": width,
 551 |         "alto": height,
 552 |         "pinza": grip,
 553 |         "tolerancia": clamp,
 554 |         "area_utilizable": round(usable_area, 2),
 555 |         "margenes": {"superior": top, "izquierdo": left, "derecho": right},
 556 |         "grabados": positioned,
 557 |         "eficiencia": round(efficiency, 1),
 558 |         "eficiencia_exacta": efficiency,
 559 |         "area_aprovechable": round(efficiency_area, 2),
 560 |     }
 561 | 
 562 | 
 563 | def _two_plate_pack(items, config, max_width, max_height, cancel_event=None):
 564 |     if len(items) < 2:
 565 |         return None
 566 |     spacing = config["espaciado"]
 567 |     allow_rotation = config["rotacion"]
 568 |     priority = config["prioridad_aspecto"]
 569 |     target_ratio = config["target_ratio"]
 570 |     orderings = ("area", "long_side", "short_side", "width", "height")
 571 |     if config["agrupar_dimensiones"]:
 572 |         orderings += ("dimensions",)
 573 |     candidates = []
 574 |     strategies = [_sort_items(items, order) for order in orderings]
 575 |     dimension_groups = {}
 576 |     for item in items:
 577 |         dimensions = tuple(
 578 |             sorted((round(item["width_mm"], 3), round(item["height_mm"], 3)))
 579 |         )
 580 |         dimension_groups.setdefault(dimensions, []).append(item)
 581 |     groups = list(dimension_groups.values())
 582 | 
 583 |     def score_bins(bins):
 584 |         if len(bins) != 2 or not bins[0] or not bins[1]:
 585 |             return
 586 |         packed_bins = [
 587 |             _best_pack(
 588 |                 bin_items,
 589 |                 max_width,
 590 |                 max_height,
 591 |                 spacing,
 592 |                 allow_rotation,
 593 |                 priority=priority,
 594 |                 target_ratio=target_ratio,
 595 |                 group_dimensions=config["agrupar_dimensiones"],
 596 |                 cancel_event=cancel_event,
 597 |                 max_ratio=config["ratio_maximo"],
 598 |             )
 599 |             for bin_items in bins
 600 |         ]
 601 |         if any(packed is None for packed in packed_bins):
 602 |             return
 603 |         boards = [_as_board(packed, config) for packed in packed_bins]
 604 |         if any(
 605 |             (config["pinza"] > 0 and board["ancho"] > board["alto"] + 1e-7)
 606 |             or max(board["alto"] / board["ancho"], board["ancho"] / board["alto"])
 607 |             > config["ratio_maximo"] + 1e-9
 608 |             for board in boards
 609 |         ):
 610 |             return
 611 |         usable_area = sum(board["area_aprovechable"] for board in boards)
 612 |         shape_ratio = max(board["alto"] for board in boards) / sum(
 613 |             board["ancho"] for board in boards
 614 |         )
 615 |         if (
 616 |             not 1 / config["ratio_maximo"] - 1e-9
 617 |             <= shape_ratio
 618 |             <= config["ratio_maximo"] + 1e-9
 619 |         ):
 620 |             return
 621 |         mixing_penalty = sum(
 622 |             len(
 623 |                 {
 624 |                     tuple(
 625 |                         sorted(
 626 |                             (round(item["width_mm"], 3), round(item["height_mm"], 3))
 627 |                         )
 628 |                     )
 629 |                     for item in bin_items
 630 |                 }
 631 |             )
 632 |             - 1
 633 |             for bin_items in bins
 634 |         )
 635 |         candidates.append(
 636 |             (
 637 |                 usable_area,
 638 |                 mixing_penalty,
 639 |                 abs(math.log(shape_ratio / target_ratio)),
 640 |                 boards,
 641 |             )
 642 |         )
 643 | 
 644 |     if 1 < len(groups) <= 8:
 645 |         for mask in range(1 << (len(groups) - 1)):
 646 |             if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
 647 |                 return None
 648 |             first_bin = [*groups[0]]
 649 |             second_bin = []
 650 |             for index, group in enumerate(groups[1:]):
 651 |                 if mask & (1 << index):
 652 |                     first_bin.extend(group)
 653 |                 else:
 654 |                     second_bin.extend(group)
 655 |             if second_bin:
 656 |                 score_bins([first_bin, second_bin])
 657 |     elif len(groups) > 8:
 658 |         all_items = list(items)
 659 |         for group in groups:
 660 |             if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
 661 |                 return None
 662 |             remaining = [item for item in all_items if item not in group]
 663 |             score_bins([list(group), remaining])
 664 | 
 665 |     for ordered in strategies:
 666 |         if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
 667 |             return None
 668 |         bins = [[ordered[0]], []]
 669 |         for item in ordered[1:]:
 670 |             if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
 671 |                 return None
 672 |             options = []
 673 |             for bin_index in range(2):
 674 |                 trial = bins[bin_index] + [item]
 675 |                 packed = _pack_order(
 676 |                     trial,
 677 |                     max_width,
 678 |                     max_height,
 679 |                     spacing,
 680 |                     allow_rotation,
 681 |                     "area",
 682 |                     target_ratio,
 683 |                     cancel_event,
 684 |                 )
 685 |                 if packed:
 686 |                     area = packed["ancho_util"] * packed["alto_util"]
 687 |                     options.append((area, bin_index))
 688 |             if not options:
 689 |                 bins = []
 690 |                 break
 691 |             _, bin_index = min(options)
 692 |             bins[bin_index].append(item)
 693 |         score_bins(bins)
 694 | 
 695 |     if not candidates:
 696 |         return None
 697 |     minimum_area = min(candidate[0] for candidate in candidates)
 698 |     area_tolerance = 0.03 * priority / 100
 699 |     eligible = [
 700 |         candidate
 701 |         for candidate in candidates
 702 |         if candidate[0] <= minimum_area * (1 + area_tolerance) + 1e-9
 703 |     ]
 704 |     _, _, _, boards = min(
 705 |         eligible, key=lambda candidate: (candidate[1], candidate[2], candidate[0])
 706 |     )
 707 |     assembled_width = round(sum(board["ancho"] for board in boards), 2)
 708 |     assembled_height = max(board["alto"] for board in boards)
 709 |     drawings = []
 710 |     offset_x = 0.0
 711 |     for plate_index, board in enumerate(boards, start=1):
 712 |         for part in board["grabados"]:
 713 |             drawings.append(
 714 |                 {
 715 |                     **part,
 716 |                     "x": round(part["x"] + offset_x, 2),
 717 |                     "y": round(part["y"] + assembled_height - board["alto"], 2),
 718 |                     "placa": plate_index,
 719 |                 }
 720 |             )
 721 |         offset_x += board["ancho"]
 722 |     area = sum(part["w"] * part["h"] for part in drawings)
 723 |     material_area = sum(board["area_aprovechable"] for board in boards)
 724 |     efficiency = area / material_area * 100 if material_area else 0
 725 |     return {
 726 |         "ancho": assembled_width,
 727 |         "alto": assembled_height,
 728 |         "pinza": config["pinza"],
 729 |         "tolerancia": config["tolerancia"],
 730 |         "margenes": {
 731 |             "superior": config["margen_superior"],
 732 |             "izquierdo": config["margen_izquierdo"],
 733 |             "derecho": config["margen_derecho"],
 734 |         },
 735 |         "grabados": drawings,
 736 |         "placas": boards,
 737 |         "eficiencia": round(efficiency, 1),
 738 |         "eficiencia_exacta": efficiency,
 739 |     }
 740 | 
 741 | 
 742 | def optimizar_piezas(
 743 |     piezas,
 744 |     config=None,
 745 |     incluir_dividida=True,
 746 |     buscar_ancho=True,
 747 |     cancel_event=None,
 748 | ):
 749 |     """Return best single-plate and two-plate layouts for measured rectangles."""
 750 |     if not piezas:
 751 |         return {"placa_unica": None, "placas_dobles": None, "errores": []}
 752 |     if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
 753 |         return {
 754 |             "placa_unica": None,
 755 |             "placas_dobles": None,
 756 |             "errores": [],
 757 |             "cancelado": True,
 758 |         }
 759 |     config = config or {}
 760 |     base_margin = float(config.get("margen", 7))
 761 |     settings = {
 762 |         "margen_izquierdo": float(config.get("margen_izquierdo", base_margin)),
 763 |         "margen_derecho": float(config.get("margen_derecho", base_margin)),
 764 |         "margen_superior": float(config.get("margen_superior", base_margin)),
 765 |         "pinza": float(config.get("pinza", 25)),
 766 |         "tolerancia": float(config.get("tolerancia", 3)),
 767 |         "espaciado": float(config.get("espaciado", 3)),
 768 |         "rotacion": bool(config.get("rotacion", True)),
 769 |         "prioridad_aspecto": min(100, max(0, int(config.get("prioridad_aspecto", 0)))),
 770 |         "ratio_maximo": min(10.0, max(1.0, float(config.get("ratio_maximo", 3.0)))),
 771 |         "agrupar_dimensiones": bool(config.get("agrupar_dimensiones", False)),
 772 |     }
 773 |     max_width = float(config.get("tambor_w", 1067))
 774 |     max_height = float(config.get("tambor_h", 1270))
 775 |     usable_width = (
 776 |         max_width
 777 |         - settings["margen_izquierdo"]
 778 |         - settings["margen_derecho"]
 779 |         - settings["tolerancia"]
 780 |     )
 781 |     usable_height = (
 782 |         max_height
 783 |         - settings["margen_superior"]
 784 |         - settings["pinza"]
 785 |         - settings["tolerancia"]
 786 |     )
 787 | 
 788 |     pieces = []
 789 |     for piece in piezas:
 790 |         try:
 791 |             width = float(piece.get("width_mm", 0))
 792 |             height = float(piece.get("height_mm", 0))
 793 |         except (AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError):
 794 |             continue
 795 |         if (
 796 |             not math.isfinite(width)
 797 |             or not math.isfinite(height)
 798 |             or width <= 0
 799 |             or height <= 0
 800 |         ):
 801 |             continue
 802 |         pieces.append({**piece, "width_mm": width, "height_mm": height})
 803 | 
 804 |     errores = []
 805 |     if len(pieces) < len(piezas):
 806 |         errores.append("Se omitieron piezas con dimensiones inválidas.")
 807 |     if not pieces:
 808 |         return {
 809 |             "placa_unica": None,
 810 |             "placas_dobles": None,
 811 |             "errores": ["Se omitieron piezas con dimensiones inválidas."],
 812 |         }
 813 |     if usable_width <= 0 or usable_height <= 0:
 814 |         return {
 815 |             "placa_unica": None,
 816 |             "placas_dobles": None,
 817 |             "errores": ["Los márgenes exceden los límites del tambor."],
 818 |         }
 819 | 
 820 |     narrowest_item_bound = (
 821 |         max(
 822 |             min(piece["width_mm"], piece["height_mm"])
 823 |             if settings["rotacion"]
 824 |             else piece["width_mm"]
 825 |             for piece in pieces
 826 |         )
 827 |         + 2 * settings["espaciado"]
 828 |     )
 829 |     maximum_aspect = min(
 830 |         settings["ratio_maximo"],
 831 |         max(1.0, usable_height / narrowest_item_bound),
 832 |     )
 833 |     requested_ratio = config.get("ratio_objetivo")
 834 |     settings["target_ratio"] = (
 835 |         min(maximum_aspect, max(1.0, float(requested_ratio)))
 836 |         if requested_ratio is not None
 837 |         else math.exp(
 838 |             (settings["prioridad_aspecto"] / 100) ** 1.2 * math.log(maximum_aspect)
 839 |         )
 840 |     )
 841 | 
 842 |     unique = (
 843 |         _best_pack(
 844 |             pieces,
 845 |             usable_width,
 846 |             usable_height,
 847 |             settings["espaciado"],
 848 |             settings["rotacion"],
 849 |             priority=settings["prioridad_aspecto"],
 850 |             target_ratio=settings["target_ratio"],
 851 |             group_dimensions=settings["agrupar_dimensiones"],
 852 |             cancel_event=cancel_event,
 853 |             max_ratio=settings["ratio_maximo"],
 854 |         )
 855 |         if buscar_ancho
 856 |         else None
 857 |     )
 858 |     portrait_search = _best_pack(
 859 |         pieces,
 860 |         usable_height,
 861 |         usable_width,
 862 |         settings["espaciado"],
 863 |         settings["rotacion"],
 864 |         width_search=buscar_ancho,
 865 |         priority=settings["prioridad_aspecto"],
 866 |         target_ratio=1 / settings["target_ratio"],
 867 |         group_dimensions=settings["agrupar_dimensiones"],
 868 |         cancel_event=cancel_event,
 869 |         max_ratio=settings["ratio_maximo"],
 870 |     )
 871 | 
 872 |     portrait_candidates = []
 873 |     for candidate in (
 874 |         unique,
 875 |         _transpose_packed(unique, settings["espaciado"]),
 876 |         _transpose_packed(portrait_search, settings["espaciado"]),
 877 |     ):
 878 |         if candidate is None:
 879 |             continue
 880 |         board = _as_board(candidate, settings)
 881 |         if (
 882 |             (settings["pinza"] <= 0 or board["ancho"] <= board["alto"] + 1e-7)
 883 |             and board["ancho"] <= max_width + 1e-7
 884 |             and board["alto"] <= max_height + 1e-7
 885 |         ):
 886 |             portrait_candidates.append((candidate, board))
 887 | 
 888 |     ratio_safe_candidates = [
 889 |         candidate
 890 |         for candidate in portrait_candidates
 891 |         if max(
 892 |             candidate[1]["alto"] / candidate[1]["ancho"],
 893 |             candidate[1]["ancho"] / candidate[1]["alto"],
 894 |         )
 895 |         <= settings["ratio_maximo"] + 1e-9
 896 |     ]
 897 |     if not ratio_safe_candidates and settings["ratio_maximo"] > 1:
 898 |         safe_target = min(1.5, settings["ratio_maximo"])
 899 |         for width, height, ratio in (
 900 |             (usable_width, usable_height, safe_target),
 901 |             (usable_height, usable_width, 1 / safe_target),
 902 |         ):
 903 |             candidate = _best_pack(
 904 |                 pieces,
 905 |                 width,
 906 |                 height,
 907 |                 settings["espaciado"],
 908 |                 settings["rotacion"],
 909 |                 width_search=buscar_ancho,
 910 |                 priority=0,
 911 |                 target_ratio=ratio,
 912 |                 group_dimensions=settings["agrupar_dimensiones"],
 913 |                 cancel_event=cancel_event,
 914 |                 max_ratio=settings["ratio_maximo"],
 915 |             )
 916 |             for option in (
 917 |                 candidate,
 918 |                 _transpose_packed(candidate, settings["espaciado"]),
 919 |             ):
 920 |                 if option is None:
 921 |                     continue
 922 |                 board = _as_board(option, settings)
 923 |                 if (
 924 |                     (settings["pinza"] <= 0 or board["ancho"] <= board["alto"] + 1e-7)
 925 |                     and board["ancho"] <= max_width + 1e-7
 926 |                     and board["alto"] <= max_height + 1e-7
 927 |                 ):
 928 |                     portrait_candidates.append((option, board))
 929 |                     if (
 930 |                         max(
 931 |                             board["alto"] / board["ancho"],
 932 |                             board["ancho"] / board["alto"],
 933 |                         )
 934 |                         <= settings["ratio_maximo"] + 1e-9
 935 |                     ):
 936 |                         ratio_safe_candidates.append((option, board))
 937 | 
 938 |     if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
 939 |         return {
 940 |             "placa_unica": None,
 941 |             "placas_dobles": None,
 942 |             "errores": [],
 943 |             "cancelado": True,
 944 |         }
 945 | 
 946 |     active_candidates = ratio_safe_candidates or portrait_candidates
 947 |     ratio_out_of_bounds = bool(portrait_candidates and not ratio_safe_candidates)
 948 |     single_plate = None
 949 |     if active_candidates:
 950 |         min_area = min(
 951 |             candidate[1]["area_aprovechable"] for candidate in active_candidates
 952 |         )
 953 |         area_tolerance = 0.03 * settings["prioridad_aspecto"] / 100
 954 |         eligible = [
 955 |             candidate
 956 |             for candidate in active_candidates
 957 |             if candidate[1]["area_aprovechable"]
 958 |             <= min_area * (1 + area_tolerance) + 1e-9
 959 |         ]
 960 |         _, single_plate = min(
 961 |             eligible,
 962 |             key=lambda item: (
 963 |                 abs(
 964 |                     math.log(
 965 |                         (item[1]["alto"] / item[1]["ancho"])
 966 |                         / settings["target_ratio"]
 967 |                     )
 968 |                 ),
 969 |                 item[1]["area_aprovechable"],
 970 |                 item[1]["ancho"] * item[1]["alto"],
 971 |             ),
 972 |         )
 973 |         single_plate = {
 974 |             **single_plate,
 975 |             "ratio_alto_ancho": max(
 976 |                 single_plate["alto"] / single_plate["ancho"],
 977 |                 single_plate["ancho"] / single_plate["alto"],
 978 |             ),
 979 |             "ratio_fuera_limite": ratio_out_of_bounds,
 980 |         }
 981 | 
 982 |     split_plate = None
 983 |     if incluir_dividida and single_plate is not None:
 984 |         split_candidate = _two_plate_pack(
 985 |             pieces,
 986 |             settings,
 987 |             usable_width,
 988 |             usable_height,
 989 |             cancel_event=cancel_event,
 990 |         )
 991 |         if (
 992 |             split_candidate is not None
 993 |             and split_candidate["eficiencia_exacta"] + 3.0
 994 |             >= single_plate["eficiencia_exacta"] - 1e-9
 995 |         ):
 996 |             split_plate = split_candidate
 997 | 
 998 |     return {
 999 |         "placa_unica": single_plate,
1000 |         "placas_dobles": split_plate,
1001 |         "errores": errores,
1002 |     }
</file>

  1 | """Suggest dimension-safe subsets with several bounded layout profiles."""
  2 | 
  3 | import os
  4 | from collections import Counter
  5 | 
  6 | from .metadata import extraer_medidas_xmp
  7 | from .packing import optimizar_piezas
  8 | from .scanner import extraer_material_fotopolimero
