 10 | def _dimension_key(piece):
 11 |     return tuple(
 12 |         sorted((round(piece["width_mm"], 3), round(piece["height_mm"], 3)))
 13 |     )
 14 | 
 15 | 
 16 | def _dimension_match_counts(pieces, tolerance_mm=0.0):
 17 |     counts = Counter(_dimension_key(piece) for piece in pieces)
 18 |     if not counts:
 19 |         return 0, 0
 20 |     if tolerance_mm <= 0:
 21 |         return max(counts.values()), 0
 22 | 
 23 |     matches = []
 24 |     for anchor, exact_count in counts.items():
 25 |         near_count = sum(
 26 |             count
 27 |             for candidate, count in counts.items()
 28 |             if candidate != anchor
 29 |             and max(
 30 |                 abs(anchor[0] - candidate[0]),
 31 |                 abs(anchor[1] - candidate[1]),
 32 |             )
 33 |             <= tolerance_mm
 34 |         )
 35 |         matches.append((exact_count + near_count, exact_count, near_count))
 36 |     _, exact_count, near_count = max(matches)
 37 |     return exact_count, near_count
 38 | 
 39 | 
 40 | def _recommendation_rank(option):
 41 |     return (
 42 |         option["_eficiencia_exacta"],
 43 |         -option["_grupos_dimension"],
 44 |         option["_dimension_group_size"],
 45 |     )
 46 | 
 47 | 
 48 | def _store_option(options_by_count, option, variants_per_count=3):
 49 |     bucket = options_by_count.setdefault(option["cantidad"], [])
 50 |     signature = (
 51 |         option.get("resolucion_dpi"),
 52 |         option.get("material_fotopolimero"),
 53 |         option["_dimension_signature"],
 54 |         option["_layout_signature"],
 55 |     )
 56 |     for index, existing in enumerate(bucket):
 57 |         existing_signature = (
 58 |             existing.get("resolucion_dpi"),
 59 |             existing.get("material_fotopolimero"),
 60 |             existing["_dimension_signature"],
 61 |             existing["_layout_signature"],
 62 |         )
 63 |         if existing_signature == signature:
 64 |             if _recommendation_rank(option) > _recommendation_rank(existing):
 65 |                 bucket[index] = option
 66 |             break
 67 |     else:
 68 |         bucket.append(option)
 69 |     bucket.sort(key=_recommendation_rank, reverse=True)
 70 |     del bucket[variants_per_count:]
 71 | 
 72 | 
 73 | def _profile_configs(config):
 74 |     max_ratio = min(10.0, max(1.0, float(config.get("ratio_maximo", 3.0))))
 75 |     balanced_ratio = min(1.5, max_ratio)
 76 |     profiles = (
 77 |         (1.0, 0, False),
 78 |         (balanced_ratio, 50, False),
 79 |         (max_ratio, 100, False),
 80 |         (balanced_ratio, 50, True),
 81 |     )
 82 |     return tuple(
 83 |         {
 84 |             **config,
 85 |             "ratio_maximo": max_ratio,
 86 |             "ratio_objetivo": ratio,
 87 |             "prioridad_aspecto": priority,
 88 |             "agrupar_dimensiones": group_dimensions,
 89 |         }
 90 |         for ratio, priority, group_dimensions in profiles
 91 |     )
 92 | 
 93 | 
 94 | def _limite_candidatos_sugerencia(piece_count):
 95 |     if piece_count <= 128:
 96 |         return 16
 97 |     if piece_count <= 256:
 98 |         return 9
 99 |     return 3
100 | 
101 | 
102 | def _store_subset_options(
103 |     options_by_count,
104 |     chosen,
105 |     dimension_groups,
106 |     profiles,
107 |     cancel_event=None,
108 |     buscar_ancho=False,
109 |     dimension_tolerance=0.0,
110 |     candidate_callback=None,
111 |     allow_singletons=False,
112 | ):
113 |     if not chosen or (len(chosen) < 2 and not allow_singletons):
114 |         return
115 |     dimension_signature = tuple(sorted(_dimension_key(item) for item in chosen))
116 |     group_count = len(set(dimension_signature))
117 |     largest_group = max(len(dimension_groups[key]) for key in set(dimension_signature))
118 |     exact_count, tolerance_count = _dimension_match_counts(
119 |         chosen, dimension_tolerance
120 |     )
121 | 
122 |     for profile in profiles:
123 |         if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
124 |             return
125 |         result = optimizar_piezas(
126 |             chosen,
127 |             profile,
128 |             incluir_dividida=False,
129 |             buscar_ancho=buscar_ancho,
130 |             cancel_event=cancel_event,
131 |         )
132 |         board = result["placa_unica"]
133 |         if board is None or board.get("ratio_fuera_limite", False):
134 |             continue
135 |         layout_signature = tuple(
136 |             sorted(
137 |                 (
138 |                     round(part["x"], 1),
139 |                     round(part["y"], 1),
140 |                     round(part["w"], 1),
141 |                     round(part["h"], 1),
142 |                     bool(part.get("rotado")),
143 |                 )
144 |                 for part in board["grabados"]
145 |             )
146 |         )
147 |         _store_option(
148 |             options_by_count,
149 |             {
150 |                 "archivos": [item["path"] for item in chosen],
151 |                 "nombres": [item["name"] for item in chosen],
152 |                 "cantidad": len(chosen),
153 |                 "eficiencia": board["eficiencia"],
154 |                 "_eficiencia_exacta": board["eficiencia_exacta"],
155 |                 "ancho": board["ancho"],
156 |                 "alto": board["alto"],
157 |                 "resolucion_dpi": chosen[0].get("resolucion_dpi"),
158 |                 "material_fotopolimero": chosen[0].get("material_fotopolimero"),
159 |                 "coincidencias_exactas": exact_count,
160 |                 "coincidencias_tolerancia": tolerance_count,
161 |                 "tolerancia_tamanos_mm": dimension_tolerance,
162 |                 "_grupos_dimension": group_count,
163 |                 "_dimension_group_size": largest_group,
164 |                 "_dimension_signature": dimension_signature,
165 |                 "_layout_signature": layout_signature,
166 |             },
167 |         )
168 |         if candidate_callback:
169 |             candidate_callback(chosen, board)
170 | 
171 | 
172 | def _best_options_for_group(
173 |     pieces,
174 |     config,
175 |     cancel_event=None,
176 |     progress_callback=None,
177 |     candidate_callback=None,
178 |     allow_singletons=False,
179 |     candidate_limit=3,
180 | ):
181 |     sort_orders = (
182 |         lambda item: item["width_mm"] * item["height_mm"],
183 |         lambda item: max(item["width_mm"], item["height_mm"]),
184 |         lambda item: item["height_mm"] / item["width_mm"],
185 |         lambda item: item["width_mm"],
186 |         lambda item: item["height_mm"],
187 |     )
188 |     dimension_groups = {}
189 |     for piece in pieces:
190 |         dimension_groups.setdefault(_dimension_key(piece), []).append(piece)
191 |     dimension_tolerance = (
192 |         float(config.get("tolerancia_tamanos_cercanos", 1.0))
193 |         if config.get("agrupar_tamanos_cercanos", False)
194 |         else 0.0
195 |     )
196 | 
197 |     profiles = _profile_configs(config)
198 |     subset_candidates = {}
199 |     minimum_subset_size = 1 if allow_singletons else 2
200 | 
201 |     def retain_subset(chosen, tolerance_candidate=False):
202 |         if len(chosen) < minimum_subset_size:
203 |             return
204 |         signature = tuple(sorted(_dimension_key(item) for item in chosen))
205 |         bucket = subset_candidates.setdefault(
206 |             (len(chosen), tolerance_candidate), []
207 |         )
208 |         if any(candidate[0] == signature for candidate in bucket):
209 |             return
210 |         group_count = len(set(signature))
211 |         largest_group = max(len(dimension_groups[key]) for key in set(signature))
212 |         bucket.append((signature, list(chosen), group_count, largest_group))
213 |         bucket.sort(key=lambda candidate: (candidate[2], -candidate[3]))
214 |         del bucket[max(1, int(candidate_limit)) :]
215 | 
216 |     probe_profile = profiles[0]
217 |     full_search_counts = set()
218 |     for sort_index, sort_key in enumerate(sort_orders):
219 |         if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
220 |             return {}
221 |         ordered = sorted(pieces, key=sort_key, reverse=True)
222 |         chosen = []
223 |         for piece_index, piece in enumerate(ordered, start=1):
224 |             if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
225 |                 return {}
226 |             trial = chosen + [piece]
227 |             probe = optimizar_piezas(
228 |                 trial,
229 |                 probe_profile,
230 |                 incluir_dividida=False,
231 |                 buscar_ancho=False,
232 |                 cancel_event=cancel_event,
233 |             )
234 |             board = probe["placa_unica"]
235 |             if board is None:
236 |                 if len(trial) in full_search_counts:
237 |                     continue
238 |                 full_search_counts.add(len(trial))
239 |                 probe = optimizar_piezas(
240 |                     trial,
241 |                     probe_profile,
242 |                     incluir_dividida=False,
243 |                     buscar_ancho=True,
244 |                     cancel_event=cancel_event,
245 |                 )
246 |                 board = probe["placa_unica"]
247 |             if progress_callback:
248 |                 fraction = (sort_index + piece_index / len(ordered)) / len(sort_orders)
249 |                 progress_callback(
250 |                     round(10 + fraction * 45),
251 |                     f"Probando acomodos: orden {sort_index + 1}/{len(sort_orders)}, "
252 |                     f"{piece_index}/{len(ordered)} grabados",
253 |                 )
254 |             if board is None:
255 |                 continue
256 |             chosen = trial
257 |             retain_subset(chosen)
258 | 
259 |     dimension_group_values = list(dimension_groups.values())
260 |     for group_index, group in enumerate(dimension_group_values, start=1):
261 |         if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
262 |             return {}
263 |         for count in range(minimum_subset_size, len(group) + 1):
264 |             retain_subset(group[:count])
265 |         if progress_callback:
266 |             progress_callback(
267 |                 55 + round(group_index / max(1, len(dimension_group_values)) * 6),
268 |                 f"Agrupando tamaños: {group_index}/{len(dimension_group_values)}",
269 |             )
270 | 
271 |     total_pairs = max(1, len(dimension_group_values) * (len(dimension_group_values) - 1))
272 |     pair_index = 0
273 |     for base_index, base_group in enumerate(dimension_group_values):
274 |         for other_index, other_group in enumerate(dimension_group_values):
275 |             if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
276 |                 return {}
277 |             if base_index == other_index:
278 |                 continue
279 |             for added_count in range(1, len(other_group) + 1):
280 |                 retain_subset([*base_group, *other_group[:added_count]])
281 |             pair_index += 1
282 |             if progress_callback:
283 |                 progress_callback(
284 |                     61 + round(pair_index / total_pairs * 16),
285 |                     f"Combinando tamaños: {pair_index}/{total_pairs}",
286 |                 )
287 | 
288 |     if dimension_tolerance > 0:
289 |         for anchor_group in dimension_group_values:
290 |             anchor = _dimension_key(anchor_group[0])
291 |             nearby_groups = [
292 |                 (
293 |                     max(
294 |                         abs(anchor[0] - candidate_key[0]),
295 |                         abs(anchor[1] - candidate_key[1]),
296 |                     ),
297 |                     candidate_group,
298 |                 )
299 |                 for candidate_key, candidate_group in dimension_groups.items()
300 |                 if candidate_key != anchor
301 |                 and max(
302 |                     abs(anchor[0] - candidate_key[0]),
303 |                     abs(anchor[1] - candidate_key[1]),
304 |                 )
305 |                 <= dimension_tolerance
306 |             ]
307 |             nearby_groups.sort(
308 |                 key=lambda match: (match[0], _dimension_key(match[1][0]))
309 |             )
310 |             chosen = list(anchor_group)
311 |             for _, nearby_group in nearby_groups:
312 |                 for added_count in range(1, len(nearby_group) + 1):
313 |                     retain_subset(
314 |                         [*chosen, *nearby_group[:added_count]],
315 |                         tolerance_candidate=True,
316 |                     )
317 |                 chosen.extend(nearby_group)
318 | 
319 |     retain_subset(pieces)
320 | 
321 |     options_by_count = {}
322 |     candidates_to_pack = [
323 |         candidate
324 |         for candidates in subset_candidates.values()
325 |         for candidate in candidates
326 |     ]
327 |     for candidate_index, candidate in enumerate(candidates_to_pack, start=1):
328 |         if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
329 |             return {}
330 |         chosen = candidate[1]
331 |         _store_subset_options(
332 |             options_by_count,
333 |             chosen,
334 |             dimension_groups,
335 |             profiles,
336 |             cancel_event,
337 |             buscar_ancho=False,
338 |             dimension_tolerance=dimension_tolerance,
339 |             candidate_callback=candidate_callback,
340 |             allow_singletons=allow_singletons,
341 |         )
342 |         if progress_callback:
343 |             progress_callback(
344 |                 77 + round(candidate_index / max(1, len(candidates_to_pack)) * 21),
345 |                 f"Validando propuestas: {candidate_index}/{len(candidates_to_pack)}",
346 |             )
347 |     return options_by_count
348 | 
349 | 
350 | def sugerir_combinaciones(
351 |     lista_archivos,
352 |     config=None,
353 |     por_categoria=None,
354 |     max_sugerencias=60,
355 |     cancel_event=None,
356 |     progress_callback=None,
357 | ):
358 |     if por_categoria is not None:
359 |         max_sugerencias = min(max_sugerencias, max(1, int(por_categoria) * 3))
360 |     search_config = dict(config or {})
361 |     pieces = []
362 |     errors = []
363 |     total_files = len(lista_archivos)
364 |     for index, path in enumerate(lista_archivos, start=1):
365 |         if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
366 |             return {"sugerencias": [], "errores": [], "cancelado": True}
367 |         measurements = extraer_medidas_xmp(path)
368 |         if measurements is None:
369 |             errors.append(f"No se pudieron leer las dimensiones de {os.path.basename(path)}.")
370 |         else:
371 |             pieces.append(
372 |                 {
373 |                     "name": os.path.basename(path),
374 |                     "path": path,
375 |                     "width_mm": measurements["ancho_mm"],
376 |                     "height_mm": measurements["alto_mm"],
377 |                     "material_fotopolimero": extraer_material_fotopolimero(path),
378 |                     "resolucion_dpi": (
379 |                         measurements["resolucion_x_dpi"],
380 |                         measurements["resolucion_y_dpi"],
381 |                     )
382 |                     if "resolucion_x_dpi" in measurements
383 |                     and "resolucion_y_dpi" in measurements
384 |                     else None,
385 |                 }
386 |             )
387 |         if progress_callback:
388 |             progress_callback(
389 |                 round(index / max(1, total_files) * 15),
390 |                 f"Leyendo archivos: {index}/{total_files}",
391 |             )
392 | 
393 |     if len(pieces) < 2:
394 |         if progress_callback:
395 |             progress_callback(100, "No hay suficientes archivos para sugerir combinaciones")
396 |         return {"sugerencias": [], "errores": errors}
397 | 
398 |     options_by_count = {}
399 |     compatibility_groups = {}
400 |     for piece in pieces:
401 |         compatibility_groups.setdefault(
402 |             (piece["resolucion_dpi"], piece["material_fotopolimero"]), []
403 |         ).append(piece)
404 |     if progress_callback:
405 |         progress_callback(16, "Agrupando archivos por resolución y material")
406 |     compatibility_groups_list = list(compatibility_groups.values())
407 |     for group_index, group in enumerate(compatibility_groups_list):
408 |         if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
409 |             return {"sugerencias": [], "errores": [], "cancelado": True}
410 | 
411 |         def report_group_progress(local_percent, message):
412 |             if progress_callback:
413 |                 fraction = (group_index + local_percent / 100) / max(
414 |                     1, len(compatibility_groups_list)
415 |                 )
416 |                 progress_callback(
417 |                     min(98, 16 + round(fraction * 81)),
418 |                     f"Grupo {group_index + 1}/{len(compatibility_groups_list)} · {message}",
419 |                 )
420 | 
421 |         options = _best_options_for_group(
422 |             group,
423 |             search_config,
424 |             cancel_event,
425 |             progress_callback=report_group_progress,
426 |             candidate_limit=_limite_candidatos_sugerencia(len(group)),
427 |         )
428 |         for candidates in options.values():
429 |             for option in candidates:
430 |                 _store_option(options_by_count, option)
431 | 
432 |     if not options_by_count:
433 |         errors.append("No se encontraron propuestas dentro del ratio máximo configurado.")
434 |         if progress_callback:
435 |             progress_callback(100, "No se encontraron combinaciones viables")
436 |         return {"sugerencias": [], "errores": errors}
437 | 
438 |     counts = sorted(options_by_count)
439 |     limit = min(max(1, int(max_sugerencias)), len(counts))
440 |     preferred = [count for count in counts if count <= 10][:limit]
441 |     remaining = [count for count in counts if count not in preferred]
442 |     slots = limit - len(preferred)
443 |     if slots >= len(remaining):
444 |         selected_counts = preferred + remaining
445 |     elif slots == 1:
446 |         selected_counts = preferred + [remaining[-1]]
447 |     elif slots > 1:
448 |         indices = [
449 |             round(index * (len(remaining) - 1) / (slots - 1))
450 |             for index in range(slots)
451 |         ]
452 |         selected_counts = preferred + [remaining[index] for index in indices]
453 |     else:
454 |         selected_counts = preferred
455 | 
456 |     suggestions = []
457 |     for count in sorted(selected_counts):
458 |         for variant_index in range(3):
459 |             options = options_by_count[count]
460 |             if variant_index >= len(options):
461 |                 continue
462 |             option = dict(options[variant_index])
463 |             for key in (
464 |                 "_eficiencia_exacta",
465 |                 "_grupos_dimension",
466 |                 "_dimension_group_size",
467 |                 "_dimension_signature",
468 |                 "_layout_signature",
469 |             ):
470 |                 option.pop(key, None)
471 |             option["variante"] = variant_index + 1
472 |             suggestions.append(option)
473 |             if len(suggestions) >= max_sugerencias:
474 |                 if progress_callback:
475 |                     progress_callback(100, "Sugerencias listas")
476 |                 return {"sugerencias": suggestions, "errores": errors}
477 | 
478 |     if progress_callback:
479 |         progress_callback(100, "Sugerencias listas")
480 |     return {"sugerencias": suggestions, "errores": errors}
</file>

 20 | class InventarioRetazos:
 21 |     CSV_HEADERS = ("id", "ancho_mm", "alto_mm", "material", "ubicacion", "notas", "creado_en")
 22 | 
 23 |     def __init__(self, database_path):
 24 |         self.database_path = Path(database_path)
 25 |         self.database_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 26 |         with self._connect() as connection:
 27 |             connection.execute(
 28 |                 """
 29 |                 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS retazos (
 30 |                     id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
 31 |                     ancho_mm REAL NOT NULL CHECK (ancho_mm > 0),
 32 |                     alto_mm REAL NOT NULL CHECK (alto_mm > 0),
 33 |                     material TEXT NOT NULL,
 34 |                     ubicacion TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
 35 |                     notas TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
 36 |                     creado_en TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
 37 |                 )
 38 |                 """
 39 |             )
 40 |             connection.execute(
 41 |                 """
 42 |                 CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS catalogo_materiales (
 43 |                     nombre TEXT NOT NULL,
 44 |                     clave TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE
 45 |                 )
 46 |                 """
 47 |             )
 48 |             legacy_materials = connection.execute(
 49 |                 "SELECT id, material FROM retazos WHERE TRIM(material) != '' ORDER BY id"
 50 |             ).fetchall()
 51 |             for row in legacy_materials:
 52 |                 original_name = str(row[1])
 53 |                 name = original_name.strip()
 54 |                 key = name.casefold()
 55 |                 existing = connection.execute(
 56 |                     "SELECT nombre FROM catalogo_materiales WHERE clave=?", (key,)
 57 |                 ).fetchone()
 58 |                 if existing is None:
 59 |                     connection.execute(
 60 |                         "INSERT INTO catalogo_materiales (nombre, clave) VALUES (?, ?)",
 61 |                         (name, key),
 62 |                     )
 63 |                     canonical_name = name
 64 |                 else:
 65 |                     canonical_name = existing[0]
 66 |                 if original_name != canonical_name:
 67 |                     connection.execute(
 68 |                         "UPDATE retazos SET material=? WHERE id=?",
 69 |                         (canonical_name, row[0]),
 70 |                     )

 72 |     @contextmanager
 73 |     def _connect(self):
 74 |         connection = sqlite3.connect(self.database_path, timeout=5)
 75 |         connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
 76 |         try:
 77 |             yield connection
 78 |             connection.commit()
 79 |         except Exception:
 80 |             connection.rollback()
 81 |             raise
 82 |         finally:
 83 |             connection.close()
 84 | 
 85 |     def listar(self):
 86 |         with self._connect() as connection:
 87 |             rows = connection.execute(
 88 |                 "SELECT * FROM retazos ORDER BY id"
 89 |             ).fetchall()
 90 |         return [dict(row) for row in rows]
 91 | 
 92 |     def materiales(self):
 93 |         with self._connect() as connection:
 94 |             rows = connection.execute(
 95 |                 "SELECT nombre FROM catalogo_materiales ORDER BY nombre COLLATE NOCASE"
 96 |             ).fetchall()
 97 |         return [row[0] for row in rows]
 98 | 
 99 |     def ubicaciones(self):
100 |         """Return distinct non-empty storage locations for autocomplete."""
101 |         with self._connect() as connection:
102 |             rows = connection.execute(
103 |                 """
104 |                 SELECT DISTINCT TRIM(ubicacion) AS ubicacion
105 |                 FROM retazos
106 |                 WHERE TRIM(ubicacion) != ''
107 |                 ORDER BY ubicacion COLLATE NOCASE
108 |                 """
109 |             ).fetchall()
110 |         return [row[0] for row in rows if row[0]]
111 | 
112 |     @staticmethod
113 |     def _normalizar_material(material):
114 |         name = str(material or "").strip()
115 |         if not name:
116 |             raise ValueError("Selecciona un material del catálogo.")
117 |         return name, name.casefold()
118 | 
119 |     @classmethod
120 |     def _resolver_material(cls, connection, material):
121 |         name, key = cls._normalizar_material(material)
122 |         row = connection.execute(
123 |             "SELECT nombre FROM catalogo_materiales WHERE clave=?", (key,)
124 |         ).fetchone()
125 |         if row is None:
126 |             raise ValueError(
127 |                 f"El material '{name}' no está dado de alta en el catálogo."
128 |             )
129 |         return row[0]
130 | 
131 |     def agregar_material(self, material):
132 |         name, key = self._normalizar_material(material)
133 |         with self._connect() as connection:
134 |             if connection.execute(
135 |                 "SELECT 1 FROM catalogo_materiales WHERE clave=?", (key,)
136 |             ).fetchone():
137 |                 raise ValueError(f"El material '{name}' ya está en el catálogo.")
138 |             connection.execute(
139 |                 "INSERT INTO catalogo_materiales (nombre, clave) VALUES (?, ?)",
140 |                 (name, key),
141 |             )
142 |         return name
143 | 
144 |     def actualizar_material(self, material_actual, nuevo_nombre):
145 |         old_name, old_key = self._normalizar_material(material_actual)
146 |         new_name, new_key = self._normalizar_material(nuevo_nombre)
147 |         with self._connect() as connection:
148 |             row = connection.execute(
149 |                 "SELECT nombre FROM catalogo_materiales WHERE clave=?", (old_key,)
150 |             ).fetchone()
151 |             if row is None:
152 |                 raise KeyError(f"No existe el material '{old_name}'.")
153 |             canonical_old = row[0]
154 |             conflict = connection.execute(
155 |                 "SELECT nombre FROM catalogo_materiales WHERE clave=?", (new_key,)
156 |             ).fetchone()
157 |             if conflict is not None and new_key != old_key:
158 |                 raise ValueError(f"El material '{conflict[0]}' ya está en el catálogo.")
159 |             connection.execute(
160 |                 "UPDATE catalogo_materiales SET nombre=?, clave=? WHERE clave=?",
161 |                 (new_name, new_key, old_key),
162 |             )
163 |             connection.execute(
164 |                 "UPDATE retazos SET material=? WHERE material=?",
165 |                 (new_name, canonical_old),
166 |             )
167 |         return new_name
168 | 
169 |     def eliminar_material(self, material):
170 |         name, key = self._normalizar_material(material)
171 |         with self._connect() as connection:
172 |             row = connection.execute(
173 |                 "SELECT nombre FROM catalogo_materiales WHERE clave=?", (key,)
174 |             ).fetchone()
175 |             if row is None:
176 |                 raise KeyError(f"No existe el material '{name}'.")
177 |             canonical_name = row[0]
178 |             used_count = connection.execute(
179 |                 "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM retazos WHERE material=?", (canonical_name,)
180 |             ).fetchone()[0]
181 |             if used_count:
182 |                 raise ValueError(
183 |                     f"No se puede eliminar '{canonical_name}': está asignado a "
184 |                     f"{used_count} retazo(s)."
185 |                 )
186 |             connection.execute(
187 |                 "DELETE FROM catalogo_materiales WHERE clave=?", (key,)
188 |             )
189 | 
190 |     def agregar(self, ancho_mm, alto_mm, material, ubicacion="", notas=""):
191 |         ids = self.agregar_lote(
192 |             ancho_mm=ancho_mm,
193 |             alto_mm=alto_mm,
194 |             material=material,
195 |             ubicacion=ubicacion,
196 |             notas=notas,
197 |             cantidad=1,
198 |         )
199 |         return ids[0]
200 | 
201 |     def agregar_lote(
202 |         self, ancho_mm, alto_mm, material, ubicacion="", notas="", cantidad=1
203 |     ):
204 |         """Insert one or more identical remnants atomically and return their assigned IDs."""
205 |         width = float(ancho_mm)
206 |         height = float(alto_mm)
207 |         count = int(cantidad)
208 |         if not math.isfinite(width) or not math.isfinite(height) or width <= 0 or height <= 0:
209 |             raise ValueError("Las dimensiones deben ser números positivos en milímetros.")
210 |         if count < 1 or count > 100:
211 |             raise ValueError("La cantidad de copias debe estar entre 1 y 100.")
212 |         clean_location = str(ubicacion or "").strip()
213 |         clean_notes = str(notas or "").strip()
214 |         inserted_ids = []
215 |         with self._connect() as connection:
216 |             canonical_material = self._resolver_material(connection, material)
217 |             for _ in range(count):
218 |                 cursor = connection.execute(
219 |                     """INSERT INTO retazos (ancho_mm, alto_mm, material, ubicacion, notas)
220 |                        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
221 |                     (width, height, canonical_material, clean_location, clean_notes),
222 |                 )
223 |                 inserted_ids.append(int(cursor.lastrowid))
224 |         return inserted_ids
225 | 
226 |     def actualizar(self, remnant_id, ancho_mm, alto_mm, material, ubicacion="", notas=""):
227 |         width = float(ancho_mm)
228 |         height = float(alto_mm)
229 |         if not math.isfinite(width) or not math.isfinite(height) or width <= 0 or height <= 0:
230 |             raise ValueError("Las dimensiones deben ser números positivos en milímetros.")
231 |         with self._connect() as connection:
232 |             material = self._resolver_material(connection, material)
233 |             cursor = connection.execute(
234 |                 """UPDATE retazos SET ancho_mm=?, alto_mm=?, material=?, ubicacion=?, notas=?
235 |                    WHERE id=?""",
236 |                 (
237 |                     width,
238 |                     height,
239 |                     material,
240 |                     str(ubicacion).strip(),
241 |                     str(notas).strip(),
242 |                     int(remnant_id),
243 |                 ),
244 |             )
245 |             if cursor.rowcount == 0:
246 |                 raise KeyError(f"No existe el retazo {remnant_id}.")
247 | 
248 |     def eliminar(self, remnant_id):
249 |         with self._connect() as connection:
250 |             cursor = connection.execute(
251 |                 "DELETE FROM retazos WHERE id=?", (int(remnant_id),)
252 |             )
253 |             if cursor.rowcount == 0:
254 |                 raise KeyError(f"No existe el retazo {remnant_id}.")

256 |     def exportar_csv(self, csv_path):
257 |         """Export current remnants (or a ready-to-fill header template if empty) to CSV."""
258 |         remnants = self.listar()
259 |         destination = Path(csv_path)
260 |         destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
261 |         with open(destination, "w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as target:
262 |             writer = csv.writer(target, delimiter=";")
263 |             writer.writerow(("id", "ancho_mm", "alto_mm", "material", "ubicacion", "notas", "creado_en"))
264 |             for item in remnants:
265 |                 writer.writerow(
266 |                     (
267 |                         item["id"],
268 |                         f"{float(item['ancho_mm']):g}",
269 |                         f"{float(item['alto_mm']):g}",
270 |                         item["material"],
271 |                         item.get("ubicacion", ""),
272 |                         item.get("notas", ""),
273 |                         item.get("creado_en", ""),
274 |                     )
275 |                 )
276 |         return len(remnants)
277 | 
278 |     def importar_csv(self, csv_path):
279 |         with open(csv_path, "r", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as source:
280 |             sample = source.read(4096)
281 |             source.seek(0)
282 |             try:
283 |                 dialect = csv.Sniffer().sniff(sample, delimiters=",;")
284 |             except csv.Error:
285 |                 dialect = csv.excel
286 |             reader = csv.DictReader(source, dialect=dialect)
287 |             headers = {}
288 |             for header in reader.fieldnames or []:
289 |                 normalized = str(header or "").strip().casefold().replace(" ", "_")
290 |                 if not normalized:
291 |                     raise ValueError("El CSV contiene un encabezado vacío.")
292 |                 if normalized in headers:
293 |                     raise ValueError(f"El CSV repite el encabezado '{header}'.")
294 |                 headers[normalized] = header
295 | 
296 |             width_header = headers.get("ancho_mm") or headers.get("width_mm")
297 |             height_header = headers.get("alto_mm") or headers.get("height_mm")
298 |             material_header = headers.get("material")
299 |             if width_header is None or height_header is None or material_header is None:
300 |                 raise ValueError(
301 |                     "El CSV debe incluir las columnas ancho_mm, alto_mm y material. "
302 |                     "El orden de las columnas es libre."
303 |                 )
304 |             location_header = headers.get("ubicacion") or headers.get("location")
305 |             notes_header = headers.get("notas") or headers.get("notes")
306 |             rows = []
307 |             errors = []
308 |             for line_number, row in enumerate(reader, start=2):
309 |                 if not any(str(value or "").strip() for value in row.values()):
310 |                     continue
311 |                 if None in row:
312 |                     errors.append(f"Fila {line_number}: hay más valores que encabezados.")
313 |                     continue
314 |                 try:
315 |                     width = self._parse_number(row.get(width_header))
316 |                     height = self._parse_number(row.get(height_header))
317 |                     material = str(row.get(material_header) or "").strip()
318 |                     location = str(row.get(location_header) or "").strip()
319 |                     notes = str(row.get(notes_header) or "").strip()
320 |                     if not math.isfinite(width) or not math.isfinite(height):
321 |                         raise ValueError("Las medidas deben ser finitas.")
322 |                     if width <= 0 or height <= 0:
323 |                         raise ValueError("Las medidas deben ser mayores que cero.")
324 |                     if not material:
325 |                         raise ValueError("Falta el material.")
326 |                     rows.append(
327 |                         (line_number, width, height, material, location, notes)
328 |                     )
329 |                 except (TypeError, ValueError):
330 |                     errors.append(
331 |                         f"Fila {line_number}: ancho_mm y alto_mm deben ser números "
332 |                         "positivos y material no puede quedar vacío."
333 |                     )
334 |             if errors:
335 |                 raise ValueError("CSV no importado. " + " ".join(errors[:20]))
336 |             if not rows:
337 |                 raise ValueError("El CSV no contiene retazos válidos para importar.")
338 | 
339 |         with self._connect() as connection:
340 |             resolved_rows = []
341 |             for line_number, width, height, material, location, notes in rows:
342 |                 try:
343 |                     canonical_material = self._resolver_material(connection, material)
344 |                 except ValueError as error:
345 |                     errors.append(f"Fila {line_number}: {error}")
346 |                     continue
347 |                 resolved_rows.append(
348 |                     (width, height, canonical_material, location, notes)
349 |                 )
350 |             if errors:
351 |                 raise ValueError("CSV no importado. " + " ".join(errors[:20]))
352 |             connection.executemany(
353 |                 """INSERT INTO retazos (ancho_mm, alto_mm, material, ubicacion, notas)
354 |                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
355 |                 resolved_rows,
356 |             )
357 |         return len(resolved_rows)
358 | 
359 |     @staticmethod
360 |     def _parse_number(value):
361 |         text = str(value or "").strip().replace(" ", "")
362 |         if "," in text and "." not in text:
363 |             text = text.replace(",", ".")
364 |         return float(text)
365 | 
366 | 
367 | def calcular_cortes_sobrantes_retazo(remnant, layout_result=None):
368 |     """Calculate remaining rectangle dimensions after a horizontal or vertical guillotine cut."""
369 |     if not layout_result:
370 |         return None
371 |     try:
372 |         raw_w = float(remnant["ancho_mm"])
373 |         raw_h = float(remnant["alto_mm"])
374 |         plate_w, plate_h = sorted((raw_w, raw_h))
375 |         cut_w = float(layout_result.get("ancho_acomodo", layout_result.get("ancho", 0)))
376 |         cut_h = float(layout_result.get("alto_acomodo", layout_result.get("alto", 0)))
377 |     except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError):
378 |         return None
379 | 
380 |     if cut_w <= 0 or cut_h <= 0 or cut_w > plate_w + 1e-6 or cut_h > plate_h + 1e-6:
381 |         return None
382 | 
383 |     rem_w_vertical = round(max(0.0, plate_w - cut_w), 1)
384 |     rem_h_vertical = round(plate_h, 1)
385 |     area_vertical = rem_w_vertical * rem_h_vertical
386 | 
387 |     rem_w_horizontal = round(plate_w, 1)
388 |     rem_h_horizontal = round(max(0.0, plate_h - cut_h), 1)
389 |     area_horizontal = rem_w_horizontal * rem_h_horizontal
390 | 
391 |     return {
392 |         "placa_orientada_ancho": round(plate_w, 1),
393 |         "placa_orientada_alto": round(plate_h, 1),
394 |         "corte_ancho": round(cut_w, 1),
395 |         "corte_alto": round(cut_h, 1),
396 |         "corte_vertical": (
397 |             {
398 |                 "ancho_mm": rem_w_vertical,
399 |                 "alto_mm": rem_h_vertical,
400 |                 "area_m2": round(area_vertical / 1_000_000.0, 4),
401 |             }
402 |             if rem_w_vertical >= 1.0 and rem_h_vertical >= 1.0
403 |             else None
404 |         ),
405 |         "corte_horizontal": (
406 |             {
407 |                 "ancho_mm": rem_w_horizontal,
408 |                 "alto_mm": rem_h_horizontal,
409 |                 "area_m2": round(area_horizontal / 1_000_000.0, 4),
410 |             }
411 |             if rem_w_horizontal >= 1.0 and rem_h_horizontal >= 1.0
412 |             else None
413 |         ),
414 |     }
415 | 
416 | 
417 | def _resultado_sobre_retazo(board, remnant, config, pieces):
418 |     physical_width = float(remnant["ancho_mm"])
419 |     physical_height = float(remnant["alto_mm"])
420 |     width, height = sorted((physical_width, physical_height))
421 |     usable_width = max(
422 |         0,
423 |         width
424 |         - float(config["margen_izquierdo"])
425 |         - float(config["margen_derecho"])
426 |         - float(config["tolerancia"]),
427 |     )
428 |     usable_height = max(
429 |         0,
430 |         height
431 |         - float(config["margen_superior"])
432 |         - float(config["pinza"])
433 |         - float(config["tolerancia"]),
434 |     )
435 |     usable_area = usable_width * usable_height
436 |     item_area = sum(piece["width_mm"] * piece["height_mm"] for piece in pieces)
437 |     efficiency_exact = 100 * item_area / usable_area if usable_area else 0
438 | 
439 |     parts = board["grabados"]
440 |     shift_x = float(config["margen_izquierdo"]) - min(
441 |         (part["x"] for part in parts), default=float(config["margen_izquierdo"])
442 |     )
443 |     usable_bottom = height - float(config["pinza"]) - float(config["tolerancia"])
444 |     current_bottom = max(
445 |         (part["y"] + part["h"] for part in parts),
446 |         default=usable_bottom,
447 |     )
448 |     shift_y = max(0.0, usable_bottom - current_bottom)
449 |     positioned_parts = [
450 |         {
451 |             **part,
452 |             "x": round(part["x"] + shift_x, 2),
453 |             "y": round(part["y"] + shift_y, 2),
454 |         }
455 |         for part in parts
456 |     ]
457 | 
458 |     return {
459 |         **board,
460 |         "grabados": positioned_parts,
461 |         "ancho_acomodo": board["ancho"],
462 |         "alto_acomodo": board["alto"],
463 |         "eficiencia_acomodo": board["eficiencia"],
464 |         "eficiencia_exacta_acomodo": board["eficiencia_exacta"],
465 |         "ancho": width,
466 |         "alto": height,
467 |         "retazo_ancho": physical_width,
468 |         "retazo_alto": physical_height,
469 |         "retazo_rotado": physical_width > physical_height,
470 |         "area_utilizable": round(usable_area, 2),
471 |         "area_aprovechable": round(usable_area, 2),
472 |         "eficiencia": round(efficiency_exact, 1),
473 |         "eficiencia_exacta": efficiency_exact,
474 |         "aprovechamiento_retazo": round(efficiency_exact, 1),
475 |         "aprovechamiento_retazo_exacto": efficiency_exact,
476 |     }
477 | 
478 | 
479 | def _retazo_admite_material(remnant, material_fotopolimero):
480 |     if material_fotopolimero is None:
481 |         return True
482 |     return extraer_codigo_fotopolimero(remnant["material"]) == extraer_codigo_fotopolimero(
483 |         material_fotopolimero
484 |     )
485 | 
486 | 
487 | def _mejor_acomodo_retazo(pieces, config, cancel_event=None):
488 |     boards = []
489 |     for profile in _profile_configs(config):
490 |         if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
491 |             return None
492 |         board = optimizar_piezas(
493 |             pieces,
494 |             profile,
495 |             incluir_dividida=False,
496 |             buscar_ancho=True,
497 |             cancel_event=cancel_event,
498 |         )["placa_unica"]
499 |         if board is not None:
500 |             boards.append(board)
501 |     if not boards:
502 |         return None
503 |     return max(
504 |         boards,
505 |         key=lambda board: (
506 |             board["eficiencia_exacta"],
507 |             -(board["ancho"] * board["alto"]),
508 |         ),
509 |     )
510 | 
511 | 
512 | def buscar_acomodos_en_retazos(
513 |     lista_archivos, retazos, config=None, cancel_event=None, progress_callback=None
514 | ):
515 |     """Find one-plate layouts that fit inside each remnant's usable dimensions."""
516 |     from .optimizer import _leer_piezas
517 | 
518 |     pieces, errors, cancelled = _leer_piezas(lista_archivos, cancel_event)
519 |     if cancelled:
520 |         return {"resultados": [], "errores": [], "cancelado": True}
521 |     if not pieces:
522 |         return {"resultados": [], "errores": errors, "cancelado": False}
523 | 
524 |     config = config or {}
525 |     base_margin = float(config.get("margen", 7))
526 |     remnant_config = {
527 |         **config,
528 |         "margen_izquierdo": float(config.get("margen_izquierdo", base_margin)),
529 |         "margen_derecho": float(config.get("margen_derecho", base_margin)),
530 |         "margen_superior": float(config.get("margen_superior", base_margin)),
531 |         "pinza": float(config.get("pinza", 25)),
532 |         "tolerancia": float(config.get("tolerancia", 3)),
533 |     }
534 |     compatibility_groups = {}
535 |     for piece in pieces:
536 |         compatibility_groups.setdefault(
537 |             (piece["resolucion_dpi"], piece["material_fotopolimero"]), []
538 |         ).append(piece)
539 | 
540 |     results = []
541 |     total = len(retazos) * len(compatibility_groups)
542 |     checked = 0
543 |     for (_, material), group in compatibility_groups.items():
544 |         if not _validar_resoluciones(group):
545 |             message = (
546 |                 "No se pueden combinar trabajos con distintas resoluciones DPI "
547 |                 "o sin resolución verificable."
548 |             )
549 |             if message not in errors:
550 |                 errors.append(message)
551 |             continue
552 | 
553 |         geometry_cache = {}
554 |         for remnant in retazos:
555 |             if cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set():
556 |                 return {"resultados": [], "errores": [], "cancelado": True}
557 |             checked += 1
558 |             if not _retazo_admite_material(remnant, material):
559 |                 continue
560 |             physical_width = float(remnant["ancho_mm"])
561 |             physical_height = float(remnant["alto_mm"])
562 |             width, height = sorted((physical_width, physical_height))
563 |             geometry_key = (width, height, tuple(sorted(remnant_config.items())))
564 |             if geometry_key not in geometry_cache:
565 |                 board_config = {
566 |                     **remnant_config,
567 |                     "tambor_w": width,
568 |                     "tambor_h": height,
569 |                 }
570 |                 geometry_cache[geometry_key] = _mejor_acomodo_retazo(
571 |                     group,
572 |                     board_config,
573 |                     cancel_event=cancel_event,
574 |                 )
575 |             board = geometry_cache[geometry_key]
576 |             if board is not None:
577 |                 remnant_result = _resultado_sobre_retazo(
578 |                     board, remnant, remnant_config, group
579 |                 )
580 |                 results.append(
581 |                     {
582 |                         **remnant_result,
583 |                         "tipo": "single",
584 |                         "retazo_id": remnant["id"],
585 |                         "material": remnant["material"],
586 |                         "material_fotopolimero": material,
587 |                         "trabajos": sorted(
588 |                             {piece.get("trabajo", piece["name"]) for piece in group}
589 |                         ),
590 |                     }
591 |                 )
592 |             if progress_callback:
593 |                 progress_callback(
594 |                     round(checked / max(1, total) * 100),
595 |                     f"Probando retazos: {checked}/{total}",
596 |                 )
597 | 
598 |     results.sort(
599 |         key=lambda result: (
600 |             -result["aprovechamiento_retazo"],
601 |             -result["eficiencia_exacta"],
602 |             result["retazo_id"],
603 |         )
604 |     )
605 |     return {"resultados": results, "errores": errors, "cancelado": False}
606 | 
607 | 
608 | class _DeadlineCancelEvent:
609 |     def __init__(self, cancel_event, deadline):
610 |         self.cancel_event = cancel_event
611 |         self.deadline = deadline
612 | 
613 |     def is_set(self):
614 |         return bool(self.cancel_event and self.cancel_event.is_set()) or monotonic() >= self.deadline
615 | 
616 | 
617 | def _limite_candidatos_retazo(piece_count):
618 |     if piece_count <= 128:
619 |         return 16
620 |     if piece_count <= 256:
621 |         return 9
622 |     return 3
623 | 
624 | 
625 | def sugerir_combinaciones_en_retazos(
626 |     lista_archivos,
627 |     retazos,
628 |     config=None,
629 |     cancel_event=None,
630 |     progress_callback=None,
631 |     partial_callback=None,
632 |     time_budget_seconds=120,
633 | ):
634 |     """Use the normal subset recommender independently on each remnant."""
635 |     from .optimizer import _leer_piezas
636 | 
637 |     started = monotonic()
638 |     budget = max(0.1, float(time_budget_seconds))
639 |     deadline = started + budget
640 |     timed_cancel = _DeadlineCancelEvent(cancel_event, deadline)
641 |     partial_results = {}
642 | 
643 |     def ordered_results():
644 |         return [
645 |             {key: value for key, value in option.items() if key != "_ranking"}
646 |             for option in sorted(
647 |                 partial_results.values(),
648 |                 key=lambda option: (
649 |                     -option["eficiencia_exacta"],
650 |                     -option["cantidad"],
651 |                     option["retazo_id"],
652 |                 ),
653 |             )
654 |         ]
655 | 
656 |     def make_result():
657 |         elapsed = monotonic() - started
658 |         return {
659 |             "resultados": ordered_results(),
660 |             "errores": errors,
661 |             "cancelado": bool(cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set()),
662 |             "tiempo_agotado": elapsed >= budget,
663 |             "tiempo_empleado": elapsed,
664 |             "tiempo_limite": budget,
665 |             "total_grabados": len(pieces),
666 |             "propuestas_encontradas": len(partial_results),
667 |             "estrategia": "Subconjuntos del recomendador normal, adaptados a cada retazo",
668 |         }
669 | 
670 |     def report_partial():
671 |         if partial_callback:
672 |             partial_callback(make_result())
673 | 
674 |     def report_progress(percent, message):
675 |         if progress_callback:
676 |             progress_callback(max(0, min(100, int(percent))), message)
677 | 
678 |     pieces, errors, read_cancelled = _leer_piezas(
679 |         lista_archivos,
680 |         timed_cancel,
681 |         lambda percent, message: report_progress(
682 |             round(percent * 0.1), message
683 |         ),
684 |     )
685 |     if read_cancelled:
686 |         return make_result()
687 |     if not pieces or not retazos:
688 |         report_progress(100, "No hay grabados o retazos para evaluar")
689 |         return make_result()
690 | 
691 |     compatibility_groups = {}
692 |     for piece in pieces:
693 |         compatibility_groups.setdefault(
694 |             (piece["resolucion_dpi"], piece["material_fotopolimero"]), []
695 |         ).append(piece)
696 | 
697 |     base_config = dict(config or {})
698 |     stock_width = max(
699 |         min(float(remnant["ancho_mm"]), float(remnant["alto_mm"]))
700 |         for remnant in retazos
701 |     )
702 |     stock_height = max(
703 |         max(float(remnant["ancho_mm"]), float(remnant["alto_mm"]))
704 |         for remnant in retazos
705 |     )
706 |     normal_width = min(float(base_config.get("tambor_w", 1067)), stock_width)
707 |     normal_height = min(float(base_config.get("tambor_h", 1270)), stock_height)
708 |     base_margin = float(base_config.get("margen", 7))
709 |     remnant_configs = []
710 |     for remnant in retazos:
711 |         width, height = sorted(
712 |             (float(remnant["ancho_mm"]), float(remnant["alto_mm"]))
713 |         )
714 |         remnant_configs.append(
715 |             {
716 |                 **base_config,
717 |                 "tambor_w": width,
718 |                 "tambor_h": height,
719 |                 "margen_izquierdo": float(
720 |                     base_config.get("margen_izquierdo", base_margin)
721 |                 ),
722 |                 "margen_derecho": float(
723 |                     base_config.get("margen_derecho", base_margin)
724 |                 ),
725 |                 "margen_superior": float(
726 |                     base_config.get("margen_superior", base_margin)
727 |                 ),
728 |                 "pinza": float(base_config.get("pinza", 25)),
729 |                 "tolerancia": float(base_config.get("tolerancia", 3)),
730 |                 "espaciado": float(base_config.get("espaciado", 3)),
731 |                 "rotacion": bool(base_config.get("rotacion", True)),
732 |             }
733 |         )
734 | 
735 |     unique_candidates = set()
736 |     candidate_count = 0
737 |     remnant_checks = 0
738 |     estimated_checks = max(
739 |         1, len(retazos) * len(pieces) * max(1, len(compatibility_groups)) * 3
740 |     )
741 | 
742 |     for group_index, ((resolution, material), group) in enumerate(
743 |         compatibility_groups.items(), start=1
744 |     ):
745 |         if timed_cancel.is_set():
746 |             break
747 | 
748 |         def accept_candidate(chosen, _normal_board):
749 |             nonlocal candidate_count, remnant_checks
750 |             if timed_cancel.is_set():
751 |                 return
752 |             signature = tuple(sorted(piece["path"] for piece in chosen))
753 |             if signature in unique_candidates:
754 |                 return
755 |             unique_candidates.add(signature)
756 |             candidate_count += 1
757 |             for remnant_index, (remnant, remnant_config) in enumerate(
758 |                 zip(retazos, remnant_configs), start=1
759 |             ):
760 |                 if timed_cancel.is_set():
761 |                     return
762 |                 if not _retazo_admite_material(remnant, material):
763 |                     continue
764 |                 board = optimizar_piezas(
765 |                     chosen,
766 |                     remnant_config,
767 |                     incluir_dividida=False,
768 |                     buscar_ancho=False,
769 |                     cancel_event=timed_cancel,
770 |                 )["placa_unica"]
771 |                 remnant_checks += 1
772 |                 if board is not None:
773 |                     result_key = (remnant["id"], resolution, material)
774 |                     remnant_board = _resultado_sobre_retazo(
775 |                         board, remnant, remnant_config, chosen
776 |                     )
777 |                     previous = partial_results.get(result_key)
778 |                     score = (remnant_board["eficiencia_exacta"], len(chosen))
779 |                     if previous is None or score > previous["_ranking"]:
780 |                         partial_results[result_key] = {
781 |                             **remnant_board,
782 |                             "tipo": "single",
783 |                             "sugerencia": True,
784 |                             "retazo_id": remnant["id"],
785 |                             "material": remnant["material"],
786 |                             "archivos_asignados": [
787 |                                 piece["path"] for piece in chosen
788 |                             ],
789 |                             "nombres_asignados": [
790 |                                 piece["name"] for piece in chosen
791 |                             ],
792 |                             "cantidad": len(chosen),
793 |                             "resolucion_dpi": resolution,
794 |                             "material_fotopolimero": material,
795 |                             "trabajos": sorted(
796 |                                 {
797 |                                     piece.get("trabajo", piece["name"])
798 |                                     for piece in chosen
799 |                                 }
800 |                             ),
801 |                             "_ranking": score,
802 |                         }
803 |                         report_partial()
804 |                 report_progress(
805 |                     min(99, 60 + round(remnant_checks / estimated_checks * 39)),
806 |                     f"Subconjunto {candidate_count} · retazo "
807 |                     f"{remnant_index}/{len(retazos)} · "
808 |                     f"propuestas: {len(partial_results)}",
809 |                 )
810 | 
811 |         def report_group_progress(local_percent, message):
812 |             group_fraction = (
813 |                 group_index - 1 + max(0, min(100, local_percent)) / 100
814 |             ) / max(1, len(compatibility_groups))
815 |             report_progress(
816 |                 round(10 + group_fraction * 50),
817 |                 f"Generando subconjuntos normales · {message}",
818 |             )
819 | 
820 |         _best_options_for_group(
821 |             group,
822 |             {
823 |                 **base_config,
824 |                 "tambor_w": normal_width,
825 |                 "tambor_h": normal_height,
826 |             },
827 |             timed_cancel,
828 |             progress_callback=report_group_progress,
829 |             candidate_callback=accept_candidate,
830 |             allow_singletons=True,
831 |             candidate_limit=_limite_candidatos_retazo(len(group)),
832 |         )
833 | 
834 |     final_result = make_result()
835 |     if partial_callback:
836 |         partial_callback(final_result)
837 |     report_progress(
838 |         100,
839 |         "Tiempo máximo alcanzado" if final_result["tiempo_agotado"] else "Sugerencias listas",
840 |     )

<file path="cdi_optimizer/core/scanner.py" bytes="2162" lines="64" encoding="utf-8-sig">
 1 | """Discover LEN separations stored in a RIP output directory."""
 2 | 
 3 | import os
 4 | import re
 5 | 
 6 | 
 7 | _FOTOPOLIMERO_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^(?P<trabajo>.+)_(?P<material>FP(?:45|67|112))$", re.IGNORECASE)
 8 | _FOTOPOLIMERO_CODE_PATTERN = re.compile(
 9 |     r"(?<![A-Z0-9])(?:FP\s*)?(?P<codigo>45|67|112)(?![A-Z0-9])",
10 |     re.IGNORECASE,
11 | )
12 | 
13 | 
14 | def _descomponer_nombre_len(filename):
15 |     """Return job name, ink filename, and optional photopolymer type."""
16 |     name_without_extension = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(filename))[0]
17 |     job_and_material, separator, ink = name_without_extension.rpartition("_")
18 |     if not separator:
19 |         return name_without_extension, filename, None
20 | 
21 |     material_match = _FOTOPOLIMERO_PATTERN.fullmatch(job_and_material)
22 |     if material_match:
23 |         return (
24 |             material_match.group("trabajo"),
25 |             ink + ".len",
26 |             material_match.group("material").upper(),
27 |         )
28 |     return job_and_material, ink + ".len", None
29 | 
30 | 
31 | def extraer_material_fotopolimero(filename):
32 |     """Read FP45, FP67, or FP112 from a supported LEN filename."""
33 |     return _descomponer_nombre_len(filename)[2]
34 | 
35 | 
36 | def extraer_codigo_fotopolimero(value):
37 |     """Return the supported numeric material code from a filename or label."""
38 |     match = _FOTOPOLIMERO_CODE_PATTERN.search(str(value or ""))
39 |     return match.group("codigo") if match else None
40 | 
41 | 
42 | def escanear_carpeta_rip(ruta_carpeta):
43 |     """Group LEN files by the job name before the final underscore."""
44 |     if not os.path.isdir(ruta_carpeta):
45 |         return {}
46 | 
47 |     jobs = {}
48 |     try:
49 |         filenames = sorted(os.listdir(ruta_carpeta), key=str.casefold)
50 |     except OSError:
51 |         return {}
52 | 
53 |     for filename in filenames:
54 |         if not filename.lower().endswith(".len"):
55 |             continue
56 |         job_name, ink_name, material = _descomponer_nombre_len(filename)
57 |         jobs.setdefault(job_name, []).append(
58 |             {
59 |                 "nombre_tinta": ink_name,
60 |                 "ruta_completa": os.path.join(ruta_carpeta, filename),
61 |                 "material_fotopolimero": material,
62 |             }
63 |         )
64 |     return jobs
</file>

<file path="cdi_optimizer/logging_config.py" bytes="1177" lines="40" encoding="utf-8-sig">
 1 | import logging
 2 | from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler
 3 | from pathlib import Path
 4 | 
 5 | 
 6 | def configure_logging(log_directory):
 7 |     logger = logging.getLogger("cdi_optimizer")
 8 |     logger.setLevel(logging.ERROR)
 9 |     logger.propagate = False
10 | 
11 |     directory = Path(log_directory)
12 |     log_path = directory / "optimizador.log"
13 |     for handler in logger.handlers:
14 |         if isinstance(handler, RotatingFileHandler) and Path(handler.baseFilename) == log_path:
15 |             return logger
16 | 
17 |     directory.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
18 |     handler = RotatingFileHandler(
19 |         log_path,
20 |         maxBytes=2 * 1024 * 1024,
21 |         backupCount=3,
22 |         encoding="utf-8",
23 |     )
24 |     handler.setFormatter(
25 |         logging.Formatter(
26 |             "%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(process)d %(threadName)s "
27 |             "%(name)s %(message)s"
28 |         )
29 |     )
30 |     logger.addHandler(handler)
31 |     return logger
32 | 
33 | 
34 | def configure_logging_safely(log_directory):
35 |     logger = logging.getLogger("cdi_optimizer")
36 |     try:
37 |         return configure_logging(log_directory)
38 |     except OSError:
39 |         logger.addHandler(logging.NullHandler())
40 |         return logger

 42 |     @staticmethod
 43 |     def _metadatos_extendidos(engraving):

 46 |             "color_rgb": engraving.get("color_rgb"),
 47 |             "lineaje_lpi": engraving.get("lineaje_lpi"),

 50 |         }

 64 |     @staticmethod
 65 |     def _color_relleno_pieza(base_hex, rgb_tuple):
 66 |         base = QColor(base_hex)
 67 |         if not rgb_tuple or len(rgb_tuple) != 3:
 68 |             return base, None
 69 |         ink_color = QColor(*rgb_tuple)

 72 |             round(base.green() * 0.72 + ink_color.green() * 0.28),
 73 |             round(base.blue() * 0.72 + ink_color.blue() * 0.28),
 74 |         )

110 |     def _calcular_transformacion(self):
111 |         if not self.datos_dibujo:
112 |             return None
113 |         board_width = float(self.datos_dibujo.get("ancho", 0))
114 |         board_height = float(self.datos_dibujo.get("alto", 0))
115 |         if board_width <= 0 or board_height <= 0:
116 |             return None
117 |         area = (
118 |             self.rect().adjusted(6, 6, -6, -6)
119 |             if self.compacto
120 |             else self.rect().adjusted(52, 44, -52, -58)
121 |         )
122 |         if area.width() <= 0 or area.height() <= 0:
123 |             return None
124 |         scale = min(area.width() / board_width, area.height() / board_height)
125 |         if self.compacto:
126 |             offset_x = area.left() + (area.width() - board_width * scale) / 2
127 |             offset_y = area.top() + (area.height() - board_height * scale) / 2
128 |         else:
129 |             offset_x = (self.width() - board_width * scale) / 2
130 |             offset_y = (self.height() - board_height * scale) / 2 + 1
131 |         return board_width, board_height, scale, offset_x, offset_y

133 |     def _grabado_bajo_cursor(self, cursor_x, cursor_y):

135 |             return None
136 |         transform = self._calcular_transformacion()

138 |             return None
139 |         _, _, scale, offset_x, offset_y = transform

142 |             y = offset_y + float(engraving["y"]) * scale
143 |             width = float(engraving["w"]) * scale
144 |             height = float(engraving["h"]) * scale
145 |             if x <= cursor_x <= x + width and y <= cursor_y <= y + height:
146 |                 return index
147 |         return None

286 |     @staticmethod
287 |     def _draw_plate(painter, plate, x, y, scale):
288 |         width = float(plate["ancho"])
289 |         height = float(plate["alto"])
290 |         grip = float(plate.get("pinza", 0))
291 |         painter.setPen(QPen(QColor("#aab5ae"), 1))
292 |         painter.setBrush(QColor("#e9eeea"))
293 |         painter.drawRect(
294 |             round(x), round(y), round(width * scale), round(height * scale)
295 |         )
296 |         if grip > 0:
297 |             painter.setPen(Qt.PenStyle.NoPen)
298 |             painter.setBrush(QColor("#d77b5c"))
299 |             painter.drawRect(
300 |                 round(x),
301 |                 round(y + (height - grip) * scale),
302 |                 round(width * scale),
303 |                 round(grip * scale),
304 |             )
305 |         margins = plate.get("margenes", {})
306 |         left = float(margins.get("izquierdo", 0))
307 |         right = float(margins.get("derecho", 0))
308 |         top = float(margins.get("superior", 0))
309 |         tolerance = float(plate.get("tolerancia", 0))
310 |         usable_height = max(0, height - grip - top - tolerance)
311 |         usable_width = max(0, width - left - right - tolerance)
312 |         painter.setPen(QPen(QColor("#89978f"), 1, Qt.PenStyle.DashLine))
313 |         painter.setBrush(Qt.BrushStyle.NoBrush)
314 |         painter.drawRect(
315 |             round(x + left * scale),
316 |             round(y + top * scale),
317 |             round(usable_width * scale),
318 |             round(usable_height * scale),
319 |         )

432 |                 "   |   ".join(
433 |                     f"Placa {index}: {plate['ancho']:g} × {plate['alto']:g} mm"
434 |                     for index, plate in enumerate(datos["placas"], start=1)
435 |                 )
436 |             )
437 |         else:
438 |             self.detalle.setText(self._detalle_default)
439 |         self.resumen.setText(
440 |             f"{datos['ancho']:g} × {datos['alto']:g} mm   ·   "
441 |             f"{count} grabados   ·   {plate_count} placa{'s' if plate_count != 1 else ''}   ·   "
442 |             f"{datos.get('eficiencia', 0):g}% de aprovechamiento"
443 |         )
444 |         self.btn_resumen.setEnabled(True)

  77 | def _asset_path(filename):
  78 |     bundle_root = getattr(
  79 |         sys,
  80 |         "_MEIPASS",
  81 |         os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(__file__))),
  82 |     )
  83 |     return os.path.join(bundle_root, filename)
  84 | 
  85 | 
  86 | def _suggestion_cache_key(filenames, config):
  87 |     files = []
  88 |     for filename in filenames:
  89 |         path = os.path.normcase(os.path.abspath(filename))
  90 |         try:
  91 |             stat = os.stat(path)
  92 |             fingerprint = (path, stat.st_size, stat.st_mtime_ns, stat.st_ctime_ns)
  93 |         except OSError:
  94 |             fingerprint = (path, None, None, None)
  95 |         files.append(fingerprint)
  96 |     return tuple(sorted(files)), tuple(sorted(config.items()))
  97 | 
  98 | 
  99 | def _retazo_suggestions_cache_key(filenames, config, remnants, time_budget):
 100 |     inventory = tuple(
 101 |         sorted(
 102 |             (
 103 |                 int(remnant["id"]),
 104 |                 float(remnant["ancho_mm"]),
 105 |                 float(remnant["alto_mm"]),
 106 |                 str(remnant["material"]),
 107 |             )
 108 |             for remnant in remnants
 109 |         )
 110 |     )
 111 |     return (
 112 |         _suggestion_cache_key(filenames, config),
 113 |         inventory,
 114 |         round(float(time_budget), 3),
 115 |     )

 157 | class _WorkerSignals(QObject):
 158 |     finished = Signal(dict)
 159 |     failed = Signal(str)
 160 |     progress = Signal(str, int)
 161 |     partial = Signal(dict)
 162 |     elapsed = Signal(float)

 164 | 
 165 | class _OptimizationWorker(QRunnable):
 166 |     def __init__(
 167 |         self,
 168 |         filenames,
 169 |         config,
 170 |         cancel_event,
 171 |         variant_batch=None,
 172 |         existing_alternatives=(),
 173 |     ):
 174 |         super().__init__()
 175 |         self.filenames = filenames
 176 |         self.config = config
 177 |         self.cancel_event = cancel_event
 178 |         self.variant_batch = variant_batch
 179 |         self.existing_alternatives = tuple(existing_alternatives)
 180 |         self.signals = _WorkerSignals()
 181 | 
 182 |     def run(self):
 183 |         try:
 184 |             self.signals.finished.emit(
 185 |                 generar_alternativas_archivos(
 186 |                     self.filenames,
 187 |                     self.config,
 188 |                     self.cancel_event,
 189 |                     variant_batch=self.variant_batch,
 190 |                     existing_alternatives=self.existing_alternatives,
 191 |                     progress_callback=lambda percent, message: self.signals.progress.emit(
 192 |                         message, percent
 193 |                     ),
 194 |                 )
 195 |             )
 196 |         except Exception as error:
 197 |             _LOGGER.exception("Falló el cálculo de alternativas")
 198 |             self.signals.failed.emit(str(error))
 199 | 
 200 | 
 201 | class _SuggestionWorker(QRunnable):
 202 |     def __init__(self, filenames, config, cancel_event, background=False):
 203 |         super().__init__()
 204 |         self.filenames = filenames
 205 |         self.config = config
 206 |         self.cancel_event = cancel_event
 207 |         self.background = background
 208 |         self.signals = _WorkerSignals()
 209 | 
 210 |     def run(self):
 211 |         thread = QThread.currentThread()
 212 |         previous_priority = thread.priority()
 213 |         try:
 214 |             if self.background:
 215 |                 thread.setPriority(QThread.Priority.LowPriority)
 216 |             started = monotonic()
 217 |             result = sugerir_combinaciones(
 218 |                 self.filenames,
 219 |                 self.config,
 220 |                 cancel_event=self.cancel_event,
 221 |                 progress_callback=lambda percent, message: self.signals.progress.emit(
 222 |                     message, percent
 223 |                 ),
 224 |             )
 225 |             if not self.cancel_event.is_set():
 226 |                 self.signals.elapsed.emit(monotonic() - started)
 227 |             self.signals.finished.emit(result)
 228 |         except Exception as error:
 229 |             _LOGGER.exception("Falló la búsqueda de sugerencias")
 230 |             self.signals.failed.emit(str(error))
 231 |         finally:
 232 |             if self.background:
 233 |                 thread.setPriority(previous_priority)
 234 |

 236 | class _RemnantWorker(QRunnable):
 237 |     def __init__(
 238 |         self,
 239 |         filenames,
 240 |         remnants,
 241 |         config,
 242 |         cancel_event,
 243 |         suggest=False,
 244 |         time_budget_seconds=120,
 245 |     ):
 246 |         super().__init__()
 247 |         self.filenames = list(filenames)
 248 |         self.remnants = list(remnants)
 249 |         self.config = dict(config)
 250 |         self.cancel_event = cancel_event
 251 |         self.suggest = suggest
 252 |         self.time_budget_seconds = time_budget_seconds
 253 |         self.signals = _WorkerSignals()
 254 | 
 255 |     def run(self):
 256 |         try:
 257 |             if self.suggest:
 258 |                 result = sugerir_combinaciones_en_retazos(
 259 |                     self.filenames,
 260 |                     self.remnants,
 261 |                     self.config,
 262 |                     cancel_event=self.cancel_event,
 263 |                     progress_callback=lambda percent, message: self.signals.progress.emit(
 264 |                         message, percent
 265 |                     ),
 266 |                     partial_callback=self.signals.partial.emit,
 267 |                     time_budget_seconds=self.time_budget_seconds,
 268 |                 )
 269 |             else:
 270 |                 result = buscar_acomodos_en_retazos(
 271 |                     self.filenames,
 272 |                     self.remnants,
 273 |                     self.config,
 274 |                     cancel_event=self.cancel_event,
 275 |                     progress_callback=lambda percent, message: self.signals.progress.emit(
 276 |                         message, percent
 277 |                     ),
 278 |                 )
 279 |             self.signals.finished.emit(result)
 280 |         except Exception as error:
 281 |             _LOGGER.exception("Falló la búsqueda de acomodos en retazos")
 282 |             self.signals.failed.emit(str(error))

 319 | class _DialogoResumen(QDialog):
 320 |     def __init__(self, selected_paths, available_paths, parent=None):
 321 |         super().__init__(parent)
 322 |         self.setWindowTitle("Resumen del cálculo")
 323 |         self.resize(760, 460)
 324 |         self.setStyleSheet(
 325 |             "QDialog { background: #f4f7f4; color: #263830; }"
 326 |             "QTableWidget { background: #ffffff; alternate-background-color: #f1f5f1;"
 327 |             " color: #263830; gridline-color: #d7dfd9; border: 1px solid #cbd5ce; }"
 328 |             "QHeaderView::section { background: #e8efea; color: #263830;"
 329 |             " border: 0; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5ce; padding: 6px; font-weight: 700; }"
 330 |             "QTableWidget::item:selected { background: #dceae1; color: #203b30; }"
 331 |         )
 332 | 
 333 |         jobs_by_ticket = {}
 334 |         for path, selected in [
 335 |             *((path, True) for path in selected_paths),
 336 |             *((path, False) for path in available_paths),
 337 |         ]:
 338 |             job_name = self._job_name(path)
 339 |             ticket, name = self._ticket_and_name(job_name)
 340 |             key = ("ticket", ticket) if ticket else ("job", job_name)
 341 |             row = jobs_by_ticket.setdefault(
 342 |                 key,
 343 |                 {"ticket": ticket, "names": set(), "selected": 0, "available": 0},
 344 |             )
 345 |             row["names"].add(name)
 346 |             row["selected" if selected else "available"] += 1
 347 |         jobs = [row for row in jobs_by_ticket.values() if row["selected"]]
 348 |         jobs.sort(
 349 |             key=lambda row: (
 350 |                 row["ticket"],
 351 |                 ", ".join(sorted(row["names"])).casefold(),
 352 |             )
 353 |         )
 354 | 
 355 |         layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
 356 |         total_inks = sum(row["selected"] for row in jobs)
 357 |         layout.addWidget(
 358 |             QLabel(
 359 |                 f"{len(jobs)} papeletas/trabajos · {total_inks} tintas seleccionadas"
 360 |             )
 361 |         )
 362 |         self.table = QTableWidget(len(jobs), 3, self)
 363 |         self.table.setHorizontalHeaderLabels(
 364 |             ["Papeleta física", "Trabajo", "Tintas seleccionadas"]
 365 |         )
 366 |         self.table.setEditTriggers(QTableWidget.EditTrigger.NoEditTriggers)
 367 |         self.table.setSelectionBehavior(QTableWidget.SelectionBehavior.SelectRows)
 368 |         self.table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
 369 |         self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(
 370 |             0, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents
 371 |         )
 372 |         self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(
 373 |             1, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.Stretch
 374 |         )
 375 |         self.table.horizontalHeader().setSectionResizeMode(
 376 |             2, QHeaderView.ResizeMode.ResizeToContents
 377 |         )
 378 | 
 379 |         for row_index, job in enumerate(jobs):
 380 |             names = " / ".join(sorted(job["names"], key=str.casefold))
 381 |             values = (
 382 |                 job["ticket"] or "Sin papeleta",
 383 |                 names,
 384 |                 f"{job['selected']} de {job['available'] or job['selected']}",
 385 |             )
 386 |             for column, value in enumerate(values):
 387 |                 self.table.setItem(row_index, column, QTableWidgetItem(value))
 388 |         layout.addWidget(self.table, 1)
 389 | 
 390 |         buttons = QDialogButtonBox(QDialogButtonBox.StandardButton.Close)
 391 |         buttons.rejected.connect(self.reject)
 392 |         buttons.accepted.connect(self.accept)
 393 |         layout.addWidget(buttons)
 394 | 
 395 |     @staticmethod
 396 |     def _job_name(path):
 397 |         stem = Path(path).stem
 398 |         parts = stem.rsplit("_", 1)
 399 |         return parts[0] if len(parts) == 2 else stem
 400 | 
 401 |     @staticmethod
 402 |     def _ticket_and_name(job):
 403 |         match = re.match(r"^(\d{5})(?:\s*-\s*|\s+)(.+)$", job)
 404 |         if match:
 405 |             return match.group(1), match.group(2).strip()
 406 |         return "", job

 439 |     def eventFilter(self, watched, event):
 440 |         if (
 441 |             watched in (self.canvas, self.efficiency)
 442 |             and event.type() == QEvent.Type.MouseButtonPress
 443 |             and event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton
 444 |         ):
 445 |             if not self.isEnabled():
 446 |                 return True
 447 |             self.selected.emit(self.index)
 448 |             return True
 449 |         return super().eventFilter(watched, event)
 450 | 
 451 |     def mousePressEvent(self, event):
 452 |         if event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
 453 |             if not self.isEnabled():
 454 |                 event.accept()
 455 |                 return
 456 |             self.selected.emit(self.index)
 457 |             event.accept()
 458 |             return
 459 |         super().mousePressEvent(event)
 460 | 
 461 |     def keyPressEvent(self, event):
 462 |         if event.key() in (
 463 |             Qt.Key.Key_Space,
 464 |             Qt.Key.Key_Return,
 465 |             Qt.Key.Key_Enter,
 466 |         ):
 467 |             if not self.isEnabled():
 468 |                 event.accept()
 469 |                 return
 470 |             self.selected.emit(self.index)
 471 |             event.accept()
 472 |             return
 473 |         super().keyPressEvent(event)
 474 | 
 475 |     def set_selected(self, selected):
 476 |         self.setProperty("selected", selected)
 477 |         self.style().unpolish(self)
 478 |         self.style().polish(self)
 479 |         self.update()
