 870 |     def cargar_archivos_reales(self):
 871 |         self._cancelar_precalculo_sugerencias()
 872 |         self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()
 873 |         selected_paths = set(self._obtener_archivos_seleccionados())
 874 |         self._ink_tooltip_timer.stop()
 875 |         self._hovered_ink_item = None
 876 |         self._ink_measurements_cache.clear()
 877 |         saved_paths = self.settings.value("archivos_seleccionados", [])
 878 |         if isinstance(saved_paths, str):
 879 |             saved_paths = [saved_paths]
 880 |         selected_paths.update(saved_paths or [])
 881 |         self.alternatives = []
 882 |         self.preview_result.actualizar_grafico(None)
 883 |         self.options_count.setText("Calcula para ver opciones")
 884 |         self._show_empty_options()
 885 |         self._updating_tree = True
 886 |         self.arbol_trabajos.clear()
 887 |         folder = self.settings.value("ruta_rip", "")
 888 |         self._watch_rip_folder(folder)
 889 |         if not folder or not os.path.isdir(folder):
 890 |             self.route_label.setText("Carpeta RIP sin configurar")
 891 |             self.statusBar().showMessage("Selecciona la carpeta de salida del RIP en Preferencias.")
 892 |             self._updating_tree = False
 893 |             self._actualizar_resumen_seleccion()
 894 |             self._reiniciar_historial_seleccion()
 895 |             return
 896 | 
 897 |         self.route_label.setText(folder)
 898 |         jobs = escanear_carpeta_rip(folder)
 899 |         available_paths = {
 900 |             ink["ruta_completa"] for inks in jobs.values() for ink in inks
 901 |         }
 902 |         selected_paths.intersection_update(available_paths)
 903 |         materials = {}
 904 |         for job_name, inks in jobs.items():
 905 |             for ink in inks:
 906 |                 material = ink["material_fotopolimero"]
 907 |                 materials.setdefault(material, {}).setdefault(job_name, []).append(ink)
 908 | 
 909 |         material_order = {"FP45": 0, "FP67": 1, "FP112": 2, None: 3}
 910 |         ink_count = 0
 911 |         for material, jobs_by_name in sorted(
 912 |             materials.items(),
 913 |             key=lambda item: (material_order.get(item[0], 4), item[0] or ""),
 914 |         ):
 915 |             material_name = material or "Sin tipo de fotopolímero"
 916 |             material_item = QTreeWidgetItem(self.arbol_trabajos, [material_name])
 917 |             material_item.setFlags(
 918 |                 material_item.flags() & ~Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsSelectable
 919 |             )
 920 |             for job_name, inks in jobs_by_name.items():
 921 |                 parent = QTreeWidgetItem(material_item, [job_name])
 922 |                 parent.setFlags(parent.flags() | Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsUserCheckable)
 923 |                 parent.setCheckState(0, Qt.CheckState.Unchecked)
 924 |                 for ink in inks:
 925 |                     child = QTreeWidgetItem(parent, [ink["nombre_tinta"]])
 926 |                     child.setFlags(child.flags() | Qt.ItemFlag.ItemIsUserCheckable)
 927 |                     child.setCheckState(0, Qt.CheckState.Unchecked)
 928 |                     child.setData(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, ink["ruta_completa"])
 929 |                     if ink["ruta_completa"] in selected_paths:
 930 |                         child.setCheckState(0, Qt.CheckState.Checked)
 931 |                     ink_count += 1
 932 |                 self._actualizar_estado_trabajo(parent)
 933 |                 parent.setExpanded(True)
 934 |             material_item.setExpanded(True)
 935 |         self._updating_tree = False
 936 |         self._filtrar_trabajos(self.search.text())
 937 |         self._actualizar_resumen_seleccion()
 938 |         self._reiniciar_historial_seleccion()
 939 |         self.statusBar().showMessage(f"{len(jobs)} trabajos · {ink_count} archivos LEN encontrados")
 940 | 
 941 |     def _iterar_trabajos(self):
 942 |         for material_index in range(self.arbol_trabajos.topLevelItemCount()):
 943 |             material = self.arbol_trabajos.topLevelItem(material_index)
 944 |             for job_index in range(material.childCount()):
 945 |                 yield material.child(job_index)
 946 | 
 947 |     def _iterar_tintas(self):
 948 |         for parent in self._iterar_trabajos():
 949 |             for child_index in range(parent.childCount()):
 950 |                 yield parent.child(child_index)
 951 | 
 952 |     @staticmethod
 953 |     def _actualizar_estado_trabajo(parent):
 954 |         states = [
 955 |             parent.child(index).checkState(0)
 956 |             for index in range(parent.childCount())
 957 |         ]
 958 |         if states and all(state == Qt.CheckState.Checked for state in states):
 959 |             parent.setCheckState(0, Qt.CheckState.Checked)
 960 |         elif any(state != Qt.CheckState.Unchecked for state in states):
 961 |             parent.setCheckState(0, Qt.CheckState.PartiallyChecked)
 962 |         else:
 963 |             parent.setCheckState(0, Qt.CheckState.Unchecked)
 964 | 
 965 |     def _programar_tooltip_tinta(self, item, column):
 966 |         self._hovered_ink_item = item
 967 |         self._ink_tooltip_timer.start()
 968 | 
 969 |     def _seleccionar_tinta_en_arbol(self, path):
 970 |         for child in self._iterar_tintas():
 971 |             if child.data(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) != path:
 972 |                 continue
 973 |             parent = child.parent()
 974 |             material = parent.parent()
 975 |             if child.isHidden() or parent.isHidden() or material.isHidden():
 976 |                 self.search.clear()
 977 |             material.setExpanded(True)
 978 |             parent.setExpanded(True)
 979 |             self.arbol_trabajos.setCurrentItem(child)
 980 |             self.arbol_trabajos.scrollToItem(child)

 984 |     def _mostrar_tooltip_tinta(self):

1025 |     def eventFilter(self, watched, event):
1026 |         user_event = event.type() in (
1027 |             QEvent.Type.MouseButtonPress,
1028 |             QEvent.Type.KeyPress,
1029 |             QEvent.Type.Wheel,
1030 |             QEvent.Type.MouseMove,
1031 |         )
1032 |         suggest_activation = watched is getattr(self, "suggest_button", None) and (
1033 |             event.type() == QEvent.Type.MouseButtonPress
1034 |             or (
1035 |                 event.type() == QEvent.Type.KeyPress
1036 |                 and event.key()
1037 |                 in (Qt.Key.Key_Space, Qt.Key.Key_Return, Qt.Key.Key_Enter)
1038 |             )
1039 |         )
1040 |         if user_event:
1041 |             if self._background_worker is not None and not suggest_activation:
1042 |                 self._cancelar_precalculo_sugerencias()
1043 |             self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()
1044 |         return super().eventFilter(watched, event)

1061 |             and self._worker is None

1063 |             and not self._close_after_cancel
1064 |         ):
1065 |             self._idle_timer.setInterval(

1079 |     def _cancelar_precalculo_sugerencias(self):
1080 |         if self._background_cancel_event is not None:
1081 |             self._background_cancel_event.set()
1082 |             self.background_indicator.setText("Cancelando precálculo…")

1084 |     def _iniciar_precalculo_sugerencias(self):
1085 |         if (
1086 |             not self.settings.value("precalcular_sugerencias", False, type=bool)
1087 |             or self._worker is not None
1088 |             or self._background_worker is not None
1089 |             or self._pending_folder_refresh
1090 |         ):
1091 |             return
1092 |         filenames = self._obtener_archivos_disponibles()
1093 |         if len(filenames) < 2:
1094 |             return
1095 |         config = self._configuracion_actual()
1096 |         cache_key = _suggestion_cache_key(filenames, config)
1097 |         if cache_key in self._suggestions_cache:
1098 |             self._suggestions_cache.move_to_end(cache_key)
1099 |             return
1100 | 
1101 |         self._background_cache_key = cache_key
1102 |         self._background_cancel_event = Event()
1103 |         self._background_requested = False
1104 |         self._background_progress = ("Preparando sugerencias…", 0)
1105 |         worker = _SuggestionWorker(
1106 |             filenames,
1107 |             config,
1108 |             self._background_cancel_event,
1109 |             background=True,
1110 |         )
1111 |         self._background_worker = worker
1112 |         worker.signals.finished.connect(self._on_background_sugerencias_terminadas)
1113 |         worker.signals.failed.connect(self._on_background_sugerencias_fallidas)
1114 |         worker.signals.progress.connect(self._on_background_sugerencias_progreso)
1115 |         worker.signals.elapsed.connect(self._registrar_duracion_sugerencias)
1116 |         self.background_indicator.setText("Precalculando sugerencias…")
1117 |         self.background_indicator.setToolTip(
1118 |             "Cálculo de baja prioridad; se cancela al usar la aplicación."
1119 |         )
1120 |         self.background_indicator.show()
1121 |         self._thread_pool.start(worker)
1122 | 
1123 |     def _on_background_sugerencias_progreso(self, message, percent):
1124 |         self._background_progress = (message, percent)
1125 |         if self._background_requested:
1126 |             self._actualizar_progreso(message, percent)
1127 |         elif self._worker is None:
1128 |             self.background_indicator.setText(
1129 |                 f"Precalculando sugerencias · {percent}%"
1130 |             )
1131 |             self.background_indicator.setToolTip(message)
1132 | 
1133 |     def _on_background_sugerencias_terminadas(self, result):
1134 |         cache_key = self._background_cache_key
1135 |         requested = self._background_requested
1136 |         self._background_worker = None
1137 |         self._background_cancel_event = None
1138 |         self._background_cache_key = None
1139 |         self._background_requested = False
1140 |         self.background_indicator.hide()
1141 | 
1142 |         if result.get("cancelado"):
1143 |             if requested:
1144 |                 self._worker = None
1145 |                 self._cancel_event = None
1146 |                 self._active_suggestions_key = None
1147 |                 self._set_busy(False)
1148 |                 self.statusBar().showMessage("Búsqueda de sugerencias cancelada")
1149 |             self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()
1150 |             return
1151 | 
1152 |         current_key = _suggestion_cache_key(
1153 |             self._obtener_archivos_disponibles(), self._configuracion_actual()
1154 |         )
1155 |         if cache_key == current_key:
1156 |             self._suggestions_cache[cache_key] = copy.deepcopy(result)
1157 |             self._suggestions_cache.move_to_end(cache_key)
1158 |             while len(self._suggestions_cache) > 4:
1159 |                 self._suggestions_cache.popitem(last=False)
1160 | 
1161 |         if requested:
1162 |             self._worker = None
1163 |             self._cancel_event = None
1164 |             self._active_suggestions_key = cache_key
1165 |             self._on_sugerencias_encontradas(result)
1166 |         else:
1167 |             self.statusBar().showMessage(
1168 |                 "Sugerencias precalculadas; listas para consultar"
1169 |             )
1170 |         self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()
1171 | 
1172 |     def _on_background_sugerencias_fallidas(self, message):
1173 |         requested = self._background_requested
1174 |         self._background_worker = None
1175 |         self._background_cancel_event = None
1176 |         self._background_cache_key = None
1177 |         self._background_requested = False
1178 |         self.background_indicator.hide()
1179 |         if requested:
1180 |             self._worker = None
1181 |             self._cancel_event = None
1182 |             self._active_suggestions_key = None
1183 |             self._set_busy(False)
1184 |             QMessageBox.critical(self, "Error de optimización", message)
1185 |         else:
1186 |             self.statusBar().showMessage("No se pudo completar el precálculo")
1187 |         self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()

1205 |     def _on_item_changed(self, item, column):
1206 |         if self._updating_tree:
1207 |             return
1208 |         if item.parent() is None:
1209 |             return
1210 |         self._updating_tree = True
1211 |         if item.data(0, Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole):
1212 |             self._actualizar_estado_trabajo(item.parent())
1213 |         else:
1214 |             checked = item.checkState(0) == Qt.CheckState.Checked
1215 |             for index in range(item.childCount()):
1216 |                 item.child(index).setCheckState(
1217 |                     0, Qt.CheckState.Checked if checked else Qt.CheckState.Unchecked
1218 |                 )
1219 |             self._actualizar_estado_trabajo(item)
1220 |         self._updating_tree = False
1221 |         self._marcar_resultados_desactualizados()
1222 |         self._recordar_estado_seleccion()
1223 |         self._actualizar_resumen_seleccion()
1224 |         self.arbol_trabajos.setFocus(Qt.FocusReason.OtherFocusReason)

1226 |     def _seleccionar_visibles(self, selected):
1227 |         state = Qt.CheckState.Checked if selected else Qt.CheckState.Unchecked
1228 |         self._updating_tree = True
1229 |         for parent in self._iterar_trabajos():
1230 |             material = parent.parent()
1231 |             for child_index in range(parent.childCount()):
1232 |                 child = parent.child(child_index)
1233 |                 if not child.isHidden() and not parent.isHidden() and not material.isHidden():
1234 |                     child.setCheckState(0, state)
1235 |             self._actualizar_estado_trabajo(parent)
1236 |         self._updating_tree = False
1237 |         self._marcar_resultados_desactualizados()
1238 |         self._recordar_estado_seleccion()
1239 |         self._actualizar_resumen_seleccion()

1255 |     def _filtrar_trabajos(self, query):
1256 |         query = query.strip().casefold()
1257 |         for material_index in range(self.arbol_trabajos.topLevelItemCount()):
1258 |             material = self.arbol_trabajos.topLevelItem(material_index)
1259 |             material_matches = not query or query in material.text(0).casefold()
1260 |             visible_job = False
1261 |             for job_index in range(material.childCount()):
1262 |                 parent = material.child(job_index)
1263 |                 parent_matches = material_matches or not query or query in parent.text(0).casefold()
1264 |                 visible_child = False
1265 |                 for child_index in range(parent.childCount()):
1266 |                     child = parent.child(child_index)
1267 |                     visible = parent_matches or not query or query in child.text(0).casefold()
1268 |                     child.setHidden(not visible)
1269 |                     visible_child = visible_child or visible
1270 |                 parent.setHidden(not visible_child)
1271 |                 parent.setExpanded(bool(query) or parent.isExpanded())
1272 |                 visible_job = visible_job or visible_child
1273 |             material.setHidden(not visible_job)
1274 |             material.setExpanded(bool(query) or material.isExpanded())
1275 |

1335 |     def _recordar_estado_seleccion(self):
1336 |         snapshot = self._snapshot_seleccion()
1337 |         if (
1338 |             self._selection_history
1339 |             and snapshot == self._selection_history[self._selection_history_index]
1340 |         ):
1341 |             return
1342 |         del self._selection_history[self._selection_history_index + 1 :]

1344 |         limit = int(self.settings.value("historial_seleccion", 25))
1345 |         if len(self._selection_history) > limit + 1:
1346 |             del self._selection_history[: len(self._selection_history) - limit - 1]
1347 |         self._selection_history_index = len(self._selection_history) - 1
1348 |         self._actualizar_estado_historial()

1472 |     def _seleccionar_rutas(self, filenames, record_history=True):
1473 |         selected = set(filenames)

1475 |         for parent in self._iterar_trabajos():
1476 |             for child_index in range(parent.childCount()):

1480 |                     0,

1482 |                 )

1491 |     def buscar_sugerencias(self):

1493 |         if len(filenames) < 2:
1494 |             return
1495 |         config = self._configuracion_actual()
1496 |         cache_key = _suggestion_cache_key(filenames, config)

1500 |                 and self._background_cache_key != cache_key

1507 |             )
1508 |             self._on_sugerencias_encontradas(
1509 |                 copy.deepcopy(self._suggestions_cache[cache_key])
1510 |             )

1523 |                     "El precálculo ya estaba en marcha; mostrando su avance"
1524 |                 )

1526 |             self._cancelar_precalculo_sugerencias()
1527 |         self._active_suggestions_key = cache_key

1538 |     def _on_sugerencias_encontradas(self, result):
1539 |         self._worker = None
1540 |         self._cancel_event = None
1541 |         self._set_busy(False)
1542 |         if self._close_after_cancel:
1543 |             QTimer.singleShot(50, self.close)
1544 |             return
1545 |         if result.get("cancelado"):
1546 |             self._active_suggestions_key = None
1547 |             self.statusBar().showMessage("Búsqueda cancelada")
1548 |             self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()
1549 |             return
1550 |         if self._active_suggestions_key is not None:
1551 |             self._suggestions_cache[self._active_suggestions_key] = copy.deepcopy(result)
1552 |             self._suggestions_cache.move_to_end(self._active_suggestions_key)
1553 |             while len(self._suggestions_cache) > 4:
1554 |                 self._suggestions_cache.popitem(last=False)
1555 |             self._active_suggestions_key = None
1556 |         suggestions = result.get("sugerencias", [])
1557 |         suggestions = [
1558 |             suggestion
1559 |             for suggestion in suggestions
1560 |             if all(os.path.isfile(path) for path in suggestion["archivos"])
1561 |         ]
1562 |         if not suggestions:
1563 |             message = "No se encontró una combinación viable con las dimensiones y límites actuales."
1564 |             if result.get("errores"):
1565 |                 message += "\n\n" + "\n".join(result["errores"])
1566 |             QMessageBox.information(self, "Sin sugerencias", message)
1567 |             self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()
1568 |             return
1569 | 
1570 |         dialog = DialogoSugerencias(suggestions, self)
1571 |         if dialog.exec():
1572 |             filenames = dialog.archivos_seleccionados()
1573 |             self._seleccionar_rutas(filenames)
1574 |             self.statusBar().showMessage(
1575 |                 f"Selección aplicada · {len(filenames)} grabados · calcula para ver el acomodo"
1576 |             )
1577 |         if result.get("errores"):
1578 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Archivos omitidos", "\n".join(result["errores"]))
1579 |         self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()

1792 |             return

1796 |         self._variant_batch = 0

1800 |         self._cancel_event = Event()

1802 |         self._worker.signals.finished.connect(self._on_calculation_finished)

1828 |     def _on_calculation_finished(self, result):
1829 |         self._worker = None
1830 |         self._cancel_event = None
1831 |         self._set_busy(False)
1832 |         if self._close_after_cancel:
1833 |             QTimer.singleShot(50, self.close)
1834 |             return
1835 |         if result.get("cancelado"):
1836 |             self.statusBar().showMessage("Cálculo cancelado")
1837 |             self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()
1838 |             return
1839 |         errors = result.get("errores", [])
1840 |         alternatives = result.get("alternativas", [])
1841 |         self._mostrar_alternativas(alternatives)
1842 |         self._variant_batch = 0
1843 |         self._more_variants_available = result.get("hay_mas_variantes", False)
1844 |         self._actualizar_estado_variantes()
1845 |         self.statusBar().showMessage(f"Cálculo terminado · {len(alternatives)} opciones geométricas")
1846 |         if errors:
1847 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Archivos omitidos", "\n".join(errors))
1848 |         elif not alternatives:
1849 |             QMessageBox.warning(
1850 |                 self,
1851 |                 "Sin acomodo viable",
1852 |                 "No hay acomodos dentro del ratio máximo y los límites actuales. "
1853 |                 "Revisa el ratio máximo, las dimensiones del tambor y los márgenes.",
1854 |             )
1855 |             self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()

1857 |     def _mostrar_alternativas(self, alternatives):
1858 |         self.alternatives = alternatives
1859 |         self._results_stale = False
1860 |         while self.options_grid.count():
1861 |             item = self.options_grid.takeAt(0)
1862 |             widget = item.widget()
1863 |             if widget:
1864 |                 widget.deleteLater()
1865 |         self.solution_cards = []
1866 |         if not alternatives:
1867 |             self._show_empty_options()
1868 |             self.options_count.setText("No se encontraron opciones válidas")
1869 |             self.preview_result.actualizar_grafico(None)
1870 |             return
1871 |         self.options_count.setText(f"{len(alternatives)} opciones")
1872 |         for index, solution in enumerate(alternatives, start=1):
1873 |             card = _SolutionCard(index, solution, self.options_group)
1874 |             card.selected.connect(self._seleccionar_alternativa)
1875 |             self.options_grid.addWidget(card, (index - 1) // 2, (index - 1) % 2)
1876 |             self.solution_cards.append(card)
1877 |         self._seleccionar_alternativa(1)
1878 | 
1879 |     def _anexar_alternativas(self, alternatives):
1880 |         if not alternatives:
1881 |             return
1882 |         selected_index = next(
1883 |             (
1884 |                 card.index
1885 |                 for card in self.solution_cards
1886 |                 if card.property("selected")
1887 |             ),
1888 |             1,
1889 |         )
1890 |         first_index = len(self.alternatives) + 1
1891 |         self.alternatives.extend(alternatives)
1892 |         for index, solution in enumerate(alternatives, start=first_index):
1893 |             card = _SolutionCard(index, solution, self.options_group)
1894 |             card.selected.connect(self._seleccionar_alternativa)
1895 |             self.options_grid.addWidget(card, (index - 1) // 2, (index - 1) % 2)
1896 |             self.solution_cards.append(card)
1897 |         self.options_count.setText(f"{len(self.alternatives)} opciones")
1898 |         self._seleccionar_alternativa(selected_index)

1900 |     def buscar_mas_variantes(self):
1901 |         if (
1902 |             not self.alternatives
1903 |             or not self._more_variants_available
1904 |             or self._worker is not None
1905 |         ):
1906 |             return

1909 |             return

1911 |         self.statusBar().showMessage("Buscando variantes distintas de acomodo…")
1912 |         self._cancel_event = Event()
1913 |         self._worker = _OptimizationWorker(
1914 |             filenames,
1915 |             config,
1916 |             self._cancel_event,
1917 |             variant_batch=self._variant_batch,
1918 |             existing_alternatives=self.alternatives,
1919 |         )

1921 |         self._worker.signals.failed.connect(self._on_calculation_failed)
1922 |         self._worker.signals.progress.connect(self._actualizar_progreso)

1926 |     def _on_more_variants_found(self, result):
1927 |         self._worker = None
1928 |         self._cancel_event = None
1929 |         self._set_busy(False)
1930 |         if self._close_after_cancel:
1931 |             QTimer.singleShot(50, self.close)
1932 |             return
1933 |         if result.get("cancelado"):
1934 |             self.statusBar().showMessage("Búsqueda de variantes cancelada")
1935 |             self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()
1936 |             return
1937 | 
1938 |         new_alternatives = result.get("alternativas", [])
1939 |         self._variant_batch += 1
1940 |         self._more_variants_available = result.get("hay_mas_variantes", False)
1941 |         if new_alternatives:
1942 |             self._anexar_alternativas(new_alternatives)
1943 |             self.statusBar().showMessage(
1944 |                 f"{len(new_alternatives)} variantes nuevas · "
1945 |                 f"{len(self.alternatives)} opciones en total"
1946 |             )
1947 |         elif self._more_variants_available:
1948 |             self.statusBar().showMessage(
1949 |                 "Este perfil no produjo variantes nuevas; puedes probar el siguiente."
1950 |             )
1951 |         else:
1952 |             self.statusBar().showMessage(
1953 |                 "No quedan variantes distintas dentro de los límites actuales."
1954 |             )
1955 |         self._actualizar_estado_variantes()
1956 |         if result.get("errores"):
1957 |             QMessageBox.warning(
1958 |                 self, "Archivos omitidos", "\n".join(result["errores"])
1959 |             )
1960 |         self._reiniciar_temporizador_inactividad()

1962 |     def _seleccionar_alternativa(self, index):
1963 |         if self._remnant_view_active or not 1 <= index <= len(self.alternatives):
1964 |             return
1965 |         for card in self.solution_cards:
1966 |             card.set_selected(card.index == index)
1967 |         solution = self.alternatives[index - 1]
1968 |         self.preview_result.actualizar_grafico(solution)
1969 |         self.statusBar().showMessage(
1970 |             f"Opción {index} · {solution['ancho']:g} × {solution['alto']:g} mm · "
1971 |             f"{solution['eficiencia']:g}%"
1972 |         )

1974 |     def cancelar_calculo(self):
1975 |         if self._cancel_event is None:
1976 |             return
1977 |         self._cancel_event.set()
1978 |         self.cancel_button.setEnabled(False)
1979 |         self.statusBar().showMessage("Cancelando cálculo…")
1980 |         if self._remnant_progress_dialog is not None:
1981 |             self._remnant_progress_dialog.set_cancelling(
1982 |                 self._remnant_partial_count
1983 |             )
1984 | 
1985 |     def closeEvent(self, event: QCloseEvent):
1986 |         self._folder_refresh_timer.stop()
1987 |         self._idle_timer.stop()
1988 |         self._cancelar_precalculo_sugerencias()
1989 |         if self._worker is not None:
1990 |             self._close_after_cancel = True
1991 |             if self._cancel_event is not None:
1992 |                 self._cancel_event.set()
1993 |             self._thread_pool.clear()
1994 |             event.ignore()
1995 |             self.statusBar().showMessage("Cancelando el cálculo antes de cerrar…")
1996 |             return
1997 |         if not self._thread_pool.waitForDone(1500):
1998 |             event.ignore()
1999 |             QTimer.singleShot(100, self.close)
2000 |             return
2001 |         watched = self._folder_watcher.directories()
2002 |         if watched:
2003 |             self._folder_watcher.removePaths(watched)
2004 |         event.accept()

  43 | def _leer_margenes_produccion(parent=None):
  44 |     """Obtain current margins, grip and cut tolerance from main window or QSettings."""
  45 |     node = parent
  46 |     while node is not None:
  47 |         if hasattr(node, "_configuracion_actual"):
  48 |             try:
  49 |                 return node._configuracion_actual()
  50 |             except Exception:
  51 |                 break
  52 |         node = node.parent() if hasattr(node, "parent") and callable(node.parent) else None
  53 | 
  54 |     settings = QSettings("MTRemozar", "OptimizadorCDI")
  55 |     return {
  56 |         "margen_izquierdo": float(settings.value("margen_izquierdo", 7)),
  57 |         "margen_derecho": float(settings.value("margen_derecho", 7)),
  58 |         "margen_superior": float(settings.value("margen_superior", 7)),
  59 |         "pinza": float(settings.value("pinza", 25)),
  60 |         "tolerancia": float(settings.value("tol_corte", 3)),
  61 |     }
  62 | 
  63 | 
  64 | def _formatear_fecha_ingreso(raw_timestamp):
  65 |     """Return human-readable date and numeric timestamp for sorting."""
  66 |     text = str(raw_timestamp or "").strip()
  67 |     if not text:
  68 |         return "—", 0.0
  69 |     for fmt in ("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S", "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S", "%Y-%m-%d"):
  70 |         try:
  71 |             parsed = datetime.strptime(text[:19], fmt)
  72 |             return parsed.strftime("%Y-%m-%d"), parsed.timestamp()
  73 |         except ValueError:
  74 |             continue
  75 |     return text[:10], 0.0
  76 | 
  77 | 
  78 | class _SortableTableWidgetItem(QTableWidgetItem):
  79 |     """Table item that sorts numerically when _SORT_ROLE is populated."""
  80 | 
  81 |     def __lt__(self, other):
  82 |         if isinstance(other, QTableWidgetItem):
  83 |             my_val = self.data(_SORT_ROLE)
  84 |             other_val = other.data(_SORT_ROLE)
  85 |             if my_val is not None and other_val is not None:
  86 |                 try:
  87 |                     return float(my_val) < float(other_val)
  88 |                 except (TypeError, ValueError):
  89 |                     return str(my_val).casefold() < str(other_val).casefold()
  90 |         return super().__lt__(other)

 231 |         QTimer.singleShot(0, self._foco_inicial)
 232 | 
 233 |     def _foco_inicial(self):
 234 |         self.width.setFocus(Qt.FocusReason.OtherFocusReason)
 235 |         if self.width.lineEdit() is not None:
 236 |             self.width.lineEdit().selectAll()
 237 | 
 238 |     def eventFilter(self, watched, event):
 239 |         if event.type() == QEvent.Type.FocusIn and watched in (
 240 |             self.width.lineEdit(),
 241 |             self.height.lineEdit(),
 242 |         ):
 243 |             QTimer.singleShot(0, watched.selectAll)
 244 |         return super().eventFilter(watched, event)
 245 | 
 246 |     def _update_material_completer(self):
 247 |         completer = QCompleter(self._materials, self.material)
 248 |         completer.setCaseSensitivity(Qt.CaseSensitivity.CaseInsensitive)
 249 |         completer.setFilterMode(Qt.MatchFlag.MatchContains)
 250 |         self.material.setCompleter(completer)

 252 |     def _actualizar_vista_previa_util(self):
 253 |         w = float(self.width.value())
 254 |         h = float(self.height.value())
 255 |         area_cm2 = (w * h) / 100.0
 256 |         plate_w, plate_h = sorted((w, h))
 257 |         cfg = self._production_config
 258 |         usable_w = (
 259 |             plate_w
 260 |             - float(cfg.get("margen_izquierdo", 7))
 261 |             - float(cfg.get("margen_derecho", 7))
 262 |             - float(cfg.get("tolerancia", 3))
 263 |         )
 264 |         usable_h = (
 265 |             plate_h
 266 |             - float(cfg.get("margen_superior", 7))
 267 |             - float(cfg.get("pinza", 25))
 268 |             - float(cfg.get("tolerancia", 3))
 269 |         )
 270 |         if usable_w <= 0 or usable_h <= 0:
 271 |             self.usable_preview.setStyleSheet("color: #b43c2f; font-size: 9pt; font-weight: 700;")
 272 |             self.usable_preview.setText(
 273 |                 f"Superficie física: {area_cm2:,.1f} cm² · "
 274 |                 "Sin área útil con los márgenes y pinza actuales."
 275 |             )
 276 |         else:
 277 |             self.usable_preview.setStyleSheet("color: #3d7665; font-size: 9pt;")
 278 |             self.usable_preview.setText(
 279 |                 f"{usable_w:g} × {usable_h:g} mm útiles "
 280 |                 f"(Superficie física: {area_cm2:,.1f} cm²)"
 281 |             )
 282 | 
 283 |     def _actualizar_hint_material(self):
 284 |         text = self.material.currentText().strip()
 285 |         if not text:
 286 |             self.material_fp_hint.setStyleSheet("color: #75847c; font-size: 9pt;")
 287 |             self.material_fp_hint.setText("Selecciona un material del catálogo.")
 288 |             return
 289 |         fp_tag = _etiqueta_fp_material(text)
 290 |         if fp_tag:
 291 |             self.material_fp_hint.setStyleSheet("color: #285b49; font-size: 9pt; font-weight: 700;")
 292 |             self.material_fp_hint.setText(
 293 |                 f"Compatible automáticamente con archivos {fp_tag}."
 294 |             )
 295 |         else:
 296 |             self.material_fp_hint.setStyleSheet("color: #a86420; font-size: 9pt;")
 297 |             self.material_fp_hint.setText(
 298 |                 "Sin código 45 / 67 / 112: solo se emparejará con archivos LEN sin etiqueta FP."
 299 |             )
 300 | 
 301 |     def _alta_rapida_material(self):
 302 |         initial = self.material.currentText().strip()
 303 |         name, ok = QInputDialog.getText(
 304 |             self,
 305 |             "Nuevo material",
 306 |             "Nombre del material (incluye 45, 67 o 112 para vincular con FP):",
 307 |             text=initial,
 308 |         )
 309 |         if not ok or not name.strip():
 310 |             return
 311 |         clean = name.strip()
 312 |         if self.inventory is not None:
 313 |             try:
 314 |                 clean = self.inventory.agregar_material(clean)
 315 |             except ValueError as error:
 316 |                 QMessageBox.information(self, "Catálogo de materiales", str(error))
 317 |         if clean.casefold() not in {m.casefold() for m in self._materials}:
 318 |             self._materials.append(clean)
 319 |             self._materials.sort(key=str.casefold)
 320 |             self.material.clear()
 321 |             self.material.addItems(self._materials)
 322 |             self._update_material_completer()
 323 |         self.material.setCurrentText(clean)
 324 | 
 325 |     def accept(self):
 326 |         mat_text = self.material.currentText().strip()
 327 |         if not mat_text:
 328 |             QMessageBox.warning(
 329 |                 self,
 330 |                 "Material requerido",
 331 |                 "Selecciona o escribe un material antes de guardar el retazo.",
 332 |             )
 333 |             self.material.setFocus()
 334 |             return
 335 | 
 336 |         by_key = {m.casefold(): m for m in self._materials}
 337 |         if mat_text.casefold() not in by_key:
 338 |             fp_tag = _etiqueta_fp_material(mat_text)
 339 |             fp_notice = (
 340 |                 f"\n\nSe vinculará automáticamente con archivos {fp_tag}."
 341 |                 if fp_tag
 342 |                 else "\n\nNota: El nombre no incluye 45, 67 ni 112, por lo que no se asociará automáticamente a archivos etiquetados con FP."
 343 |             )
 344 |             answer = QMessageBox.question(
 345 |                 self,
 346 |                 "Material no registrado",
 347 |                 f"El material '{mat_text}' no está en el catálogo.{fp_notice}\n\n"
 348 |                 "¿Deseas darlo de alta ahora mismo sin perder los datos capturados?",
 349 |                 QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes | QMessageBox.StandardButton.No,
 350 |                 QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes,
 351 |             )
 352 |             if answer != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
 353 |                 self.material.setFocus()
 354 |                 return
 355 |             if self.inventory is not None:
 356 |                 try:
 357 |                     mat_text = self.inventory.agregar_material(mat_text)
 358 |                 except (ValueError, OSError) as error:
 359 |                     QMessageBox.warning(self, "Material no añadido", str(error))
 360 |                     self.material.setFocus()
 361 |                     return
 362 |             self._materials.append(mat_text)
 363 |             self.material.addItem(mat_text)
 364 |             self.material.setCurrentText(mat_text)
 365 |         else:
 366 |             self.material.setCurrentText(by_key[mat_text.casefold()])
 367 | 
 368 |         super().accept()
 369 | 
 370 |     def values(self):
 371 |         return {
 372 |             "ancho_mm": self.width.value(),
 373 |             "alto_mm": self.height.value(),
 374 |             "material": self.material.currentText().strip(),
 375 |             "ubicacion": self.location.text().strip(),
 376 |             "notas": self.notes.text().strip(),
 377 |         }

 379 |     def batch_count(self):
 380 |         return self.quantity.value() if self._allow_batch else 1

 461 |     def _foco_inicial(self):
 462 |         self.width.setFocus(Qt.FocusReason.OtherFocusReason)
 463 |         if self.width.lineEdit() is not None:
 464 |             self.width.lineEdit().selectAll()

 466 |     def eventFilter(self, watched, event):

 474 |     def _actualizar_area_restante(self):
 475 |         w = float(self.width.value())
 476 |         h = float(self.height.value())
 477 |         area_cm2 = (w * h) / 100.0
 478 |         self.area_preview.setText(f"{area_cm2:,.1f} cm²")
 479 | 
 480 |     def accept(self):
 481 |         new_w = float(self.width.value())
 482 |         new_h = float(self.height.value())
 483 |         orig_short, orig_long = sorted((self._orig_w, self._orig_h))
 484 |         new_short, new_long = sorted((new_w, new_h))
 485 | 
 486 |         if abs(new_short - orig_short) < 1e-4 and abs(new_long - orig_long) < 1e-4:
 487 |             QMessageBox.warning(
 488 |                 self,
 489 |                 "Medidas sin cambios",
 490 |                 "Las dimensiones ingresadas son idénticas al retazo original.\n\n"
 491 |                 "Mide el sobrante físico tras el corte e ingresa las medidas reales antes de guardar.",
 492 |             )
 493 |             self._foco_inicial()
 494 |             return
 495 | 
 496 |         if new_short > orig_short + 1e-4 or new_long > orig_long + 1e-4:
 497 |             answer = QMessageBox.question(
 498 |                 self,
 499 |                 "Medida mayor al retazo original",
 500 |                 f"Las medidas ingresadas ({new_w:g} × {new_h:g} mm) superan las "
 501 |                 f"dimensiones originales del retazo ({self._orig_w:g} × {self._orig_h:g} mm).\n\n"
 502 |                 "¿Confirmas que la medida física es correcta?",
 503 |                 QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes | QMessageBox.StandardButton.No,
 504 |                 QMessageBox.StandardButton.No,
 505 |             )
 506 |             if answer != QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes:
 507 |                 self._foco_inicial()
 508 |                 return
 509 | 
 510 |         super().accept()
 511 | 
 512 |     def values(self):
 513 |         return {"ancho_mm": self.width.value(), "alto_mm": self.height.value()}

 516 | def elegir_accion_retazo_usado(inventory, remnant_id, parent=None, *, layout_result=None):
 517 |     remnant = next(
 518 |         (item for item in inventory.listar() if item["id"] == remnant_id), None
 519 |     )
 520 |     if remnant is None:
 521 |         raise KeyError(f"No existe el retazo {remnant_id}.")
 522 |     message = QMessageBox(parent)
 523 |     message.setWindowTitle("Retazo usado")
 524 |     message.setText(
 525 |         f"¿Qué deseas hacer con el retazo #{remnant_id} "
 526 |         f"({remnant['ancho_mm']:g} × {remnant['alto_mm']:g} mm · {remnant['material']})?"
 527 |     )
 528 |     remove = message.addButton(
 529 |         "Eliminar del inventario", QMessageBox.ButtonRole.DestructiveRole
 530 |     )
 531 |     update = message.addButton(
 532 |         "Actualizar tamaño sobrante", QMessageBox.ButtonRole.ActionRole
 533 |     )
 534 |     message.addButton("Cancelar", QMessageBox.ButtonRole.RejectRole)
 535 |     message.exec()
 536 |     if message.clickedButton() is remove:
 537 |         inventory.eliminar(remnant_id)
 538 |         return "deleted"
 539 |     if message.clickedButton() is update:
 540 |         dialog = DialogoDimensionesRetazo(
 541 |             remnant, parent, layout_result=layout_result
 542 |         )
 543 |         if not dialog.exec():
 544 |             return None
 545 |         inventory.actualizar(
 546 |             remnant_id,
 547 |             **dialog.values(),
 548 |             material=remnant["material"],
 549 |             ubicacion=remnant["ubicacion"],
 550 |             notas=remnant["notas"],
 551 |         )
 552 |         return "updated"
 553 |     return None
 554 |

 606 |     def _on_current_item_changed(self, current, _previous=None):
 607 |         if current is None:
 608 |             return
 609 |         raw_name = current.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) or current.text()
 610 |         self.name.setText(raw_name)
 611 | 
 612 |     def _update_compatibility_hint(self, text):
 613 |         clean = str(text or "").strip()
 614 |         if not clean:
 615 |             self.compatibility_label.setStyleSheet("color: #75847c; font-size: 9pt;")
 616 |             self.compatibility_label.setText("Escribe un nombre para verificar su código FP.")
 617 |             return
 618 |         fp_tag = _etiqueta_fp_material(clean)
 619 |         if fp_tag:
 620 |             self.compatibility_label.setStyleSheet(
 621 |                 "color: #285b49; font-size: 9pt; font-weight: 700;"
 622 |             )
 623 |             self.compatibility_label.setText(f"Compatibilidad detectada: {fp_tag}")
 624 |         else:
 625 |             self.compatibility_label.setStyleSheet("color: #a86420; font-size: 9pt;")
 626 |             self.compatibility_label.setText(
 627 |                 "Sin código 45 / 67 / 112: no se asignará automáticamente a archivos FP45, FP67 ni FP112."
 628 |             )
 629 | 
 630 |     def _refresh(self, selected=""):
 631 |         self.list.clear()
 632 |         selected_row = -1
 633 |         for row, material_name in enumerate(self.inventory.materiales()):
 634 |             fp_tag = _etiqueta_fp_material(material_name)
 635 |             badge = f"   [{fp_tag}]" if fp_tag else "   [Sin código FP]"
 636 |             item = QListWidgetItem(f"{material_name}{badge}")
 637 |             item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, material_name)
 638 |             if not fp_tag:
 639 |                 item.setForeground(QColor("#9a5b13"))
 640 |                 item.setToolTip(
 641 |                     "Este material no incluye 45, 67 ni 112; solo se usará con archivos LEN sin tipo FP."
 642 |                 )
 643 |             self.list.addItem(item)
 644 |             if material_name == selected:
 645 |                 selected_row = row
 646 |         if selected_row >= 0:
 647 |             self.list.setCurrentRow(selected_row)
 648 |         elif self.list.count():
 649 |             self.list.setCurrentRow(0)
 650 | 
 651 |     def _confirmar_si_falta_codigo_fp(self, candidate_name):
 652 |         clean = str(candidate_name or "").strip()
 653 |         if not clean or _etiqueta_fp_material(clean) is not None:
 654 |             return True
 655 |         answer = QMessageBox.question(
 656 |             self,
 657 |             "Material sin código de fotopolímero",
 658 |             f"El nombre '{clean}' no incluye el código 45, 67 ni 112.\n\n"
 659 |             "Los archivos etiquetados como FP45, FP67 o FP112 no se vincularán "
 660 |             "automáticamente con este material.\n\n"
 661 |             "¿Deseas guardarlo de todos modos?",
 662 |             QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes | QMessageBox.StandardButton.No,
 663 |             QMessageBox.StandardButton.No,
 664 |         )
 665 |         return answer == QMessageBox.StandardButton.Yes
 666 | 
 667 |     def _add(self):
 668 |         candidate = self.name.text().strip()
 669 |         if candidate and not self._confirmar_si_falta_codigo_fp(candidate):
 670 |             return
 671 |         try:
 672 |             name = self.inventory.agregar_material(self.name.text())
 673 |         except (ValueError, OSError) as error:
 674 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Material no añadido", str(error))
 675 |             return
 676 |         self.name.clear()
 677 |         self._refresh(name)
 678 | 
 679 |     def _rename(self):
 680 |         current = self.list.currentItem()
 681 |         if current is None:
 682 |             return
 683 |         raw_current = current.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) or current.text()
 684 |         candidate = self.name.text().strip()
 685 |         if candidate and not self._confirmar_si_falta_codigo_fp(candidate):
 686 |             return
 687 |         try:
 688 |             name = self.inventory.actualizar_material(raw_current, self.name.text())
 689 |         except (ValueError, KeyError, OSError) as error:
 690 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Material no actualizado", str(error))
 691 |             return
 692 |         self._refresh(name)
 693 | 
 694 |     def _remove(self):
 695 |         current = self.list.currentItem()
 696 |         if current is None:
 697 |             return
 698 |         raw_current = current.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) or current.text()
 699 |         try:
 700 |             self.inventory.eliminar_material(raw_current)
 701 |         except (ValueError, KeyError, OSError) as error:
 702 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Material no eliminado", str(error))
 703 |             return
 704 |         self.name.clear()
 705 |         self._refresh()

 863 |     def _selected_id(self):
 864 |         row = self.table.currentRow()
 865 |         if row < 0 or self.table.isRowHidden(row):
 866 |             return None
 867 |         item = self.table.item(row, 0)
 868 |         if item is None:
 869 |             return None
 870 |         return int(item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole))
 871 | 
 872 |     def _actualizar_estado_botones(self):
 873 |         has_selection = self._selected_id() is not None
 874 |         self.edit_button.setEnabled(has_selection)
 875 |         self.duplicate_button.setEnabled(has_selection)
 876 |         self.remove_button.setEnabled(has_selection)
 877 | 
 878 |     def _mostrar_aviso_temporal(self, text):
 879 |         self.feedback_label.setText(text)
 880 |         QTimer.singleShot(
 881 |             6000,
 882 |             lambda: self.feedback_label.clear()
 883 |             if self.feedback_label.text() == text
 884 |             else None,
 885 |         )

 887 |     def _refresh(self, selected_id=None):
 888 |         self._all_remnants = self.inventory.listar()
 889 |         sorting_was_enabled = self.table.isSortingEnabled()
 890 |         self.table.setSortingEnabled(False)
 891 |         self.table.setRowCount(len(self._all_remnants))
 892 | 
 893 |         for row, remnant in enumerate(self._all_remnants):
 894 |             rem_id = int(remnant["id"])
 895 |             width = float(remnant["ancho_mm"])
 896 |             height = float(remnant["alto_mm"])
 897 |             area_cm2 = (width * height) / 100.0
 898 |             area_m2 = (width * height) / 1_000_000.0
 899 |             material_name = str(remnant["material"])
 900 |             fp_tag = _etiqueta_fp_material(material_name)
 901 |             location = str(remnant.get("ubicacion", ""))
 902 |             notes = str(remnant.get("notas", ""))
 903 |             date_text, date_ts = _formatear_fecha_ingreso(remnant.get("creado_en"))
 904 | 
 905 |             id_item = _SortableTableWidgetItem(f"#{rem_id}")
 906 |             id_item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, rem_id)
 907 |             id_item.setData(_SORT_ROLE, rem_id)
 908 |             id_item.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
 909 | 
 910 |             width_item = _SortableTableWidgetItem(f"{width:g} mm")
 911 |             width_item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, rem_id)
 912 |             width_item.setData(_SORT_ROLE, width)
 913 |             width_item.setTextAlignment(
 914 |                 Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter
 915 |             )
 916 | 
 917 |             height_item = _SortableTableWidgetItem(f"{height:g} mm")
 918 |             height_item.setData(_SORT_ROLE, height)
 919 |             height_item.setTextAlignment(
 920 |                 Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter
 921 |             )
 922 | 
 923 |             area_item = _SortableTableWidgetItem(f"{area_cm2:,.1f} cm²")
 924 |             area_item.setData(_SORT_ROLE, area_cm2)
 925 |             area_item.setToolTip(f"{area_m2:.3f} m²")
 926 |             area_item.setTextAlignment(
 927 |                 Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignRight | Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignVCenter
 928 |             )
 929 | 
 930 |             if fp_tag:
 931 |                 mat_display = f"{material_name} ({fp_tag})"
 932 |                 mat_item = _SortableTableWidgetItem(mat_display)
 933 |                 mat_item.setForeground(QColor("#204e3d"))
 934 |                 mat_item.setToolTip(f"Compatible con archivos {fp_tag}")
 935 |             else:
 936 |                 mat_display = f"{material_name} (Sin FP)"
 937 |                 mat_item = _SortableTableWidgetItem(mat_display)
 938 |                 mat_item.setForeground(QColor("#9a5b13"))
 939 |                 mat_item.setToolTip(
 940 |                     "El nombre del material no contiene 45, 67 ni 112; "
 941 |                     "no se asociará automáticamente con archivos etiquetados FP."
 942 |                 )
 943 |             mat_item.setData(_SORT_ROLE, mat_display)
 944 |             mat_item.setData(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole, fp_tag or "NONE")
 945 | 
 946 |             loc_item = _SortableTableWidgetItem(location)
 947 |             loc_item.setData(_SORT_ROLE, location)
 948 | 
 949 |             notes_item = _SortableTableWidgetItem(notes)
 950 |             notes_item.setData(_SORT_ROLE, notes)
 951 | 
 952 |             date_item = _SortableTableWidgetItem(date_text)
 953 |             date_item.setData(_SORT_ROLE, date_ts or rem_id)
 954 |             date_item.setToolTip(f"Registrado: {remnant.get('creado_en', date_text)}")
 955 |             date_item.setTextAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
 956 | 
 957 |             for column, item in enumerate(
 958 |                 (
 959 |                     id_item,
 960 |                     width_item,
 961 |                     height_item,
 962 |                     area_item,
 963 |                     mat_item,
 964 |                     loc_item,
 965 |                     notes_item,
 966 |                     date_item,
 967 |                 )
 968 |             ):
 969 |                 self.table.setItem(row, column, item)
 970 | 
 971 |         self.table.setSortingEnabled(sorting_was_enabled)
 972 |         self._actualizar_contadores_filtro_fp()
 973 |         self._aplicar_filtros(selected_id=selected_id)
 974 | 
 975 |     def _actualizar_contadores_filtro_fp(self):
 976 |         counts = {"ALL": len(self._all_remnants), "FP45": 0, "FP67": 0, "FP112": 0, "NONE": 0}
 977 |         for remnant in self._all_remnants:
 978 |             tag = _etiqueta_fp_material(remnant["material"])
 979 |             counts[tag if tag in counts else "NONE"] += 1
 980 |         labels = {
 981 |             "ALL": f"Todos ({counts['ALL']})",
 982 |             "FP45": f"FP45 ({counts['FP45']})",
 983 |             "FP67": f"FP67 ({counts['FP67']})",
 984 |             "FP112": f"FP112 ({counts['FP112']})",
 985 |             "NONE": f"Sin código FP ({counts['NONE']})",
 986 |         }
 987 |         for idx in range(self.fp_filter.count()):
 988 |             code = self.fp_filter.itemData(idx)
 989 |             if code in labels:
 990 |                 self.fp_filter.setItemText(idx, labels[code])
 991 | 
 992 |     def _aplicar_filtros(self, *_args, selected_id=None):
 993 |         if selected_id is None:
 994 |             selected_id = self._selected_id()
 995 |         query = self.search_input.text().strip().casefold()
 996 |         fp_code = self.fp_filter.currentData() or "ALL"
 997 | 
 998 |         visible_count = 0
 999 |         visible_area_cm2 = 0.0
1000 |         fp_breakdown = {"FP45": 0, "FP67": 0, "FP112": 0, "NONE": 0}
1001 |         first_visible_row = -1
1002 |         target_row = -1
1003 | 
1004 |         for row in range(self.table.rowCount()):
1005 |             id_item = self.table.item(row, 0)
1006 |             width_item = self.table.item(row, 1)
1007 |             height_item = self.table.item(row, 2)
1008 |             area_item = self.table.item(row, 3)
1009 |             mat_item = self.table.item(row, 4)
1010 |             loc_item = self.table.item(row, 5)
1011 |             notes_item = self.table.item(row, 6)
1012 |             if id_item is None or mat_item is None:
1013 |                 continue
1014 | 
1015 |             row_fp = mat_item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole) or "NONE"
1016 |             fp_matches = fp_code == "ALL" or row_fp == fp_code
1017 | 
1018 |             if query:
1019 |                 searchable = " ".join(
1020 |                     (
1021 |                         id_item.text(),
1022 |                         width_item.text() if width_item else "",
1023 |                         height_item.text() if height_item else "",
1024 |                         mat_item.text(),
1025 |                         loc_item.text() if loc_item else "",
1026 |                         notes_item.text() if notes_item else "",
1027 |                     )
1028 |                 ).casefold()
1029 |                 text_matches = query in searchable
1030 |             else:
1031 |                 text_matches = True
1032 | 
1033 |             visible = fp_matches and text_matches
1034 |             self.table.setRowHidden(row, not visible)
1035 |             if visible:
1036 |                 visible_count += 1
1037 |                 area_val = float(area_item.data(_SORT_ROLE) or 0.0) if area_item else 0.0
1038 |                 visible_area_cm2 += area_val
1039 |                 fp_breakdown[row_fp if row_fp in fp_breakdown else "NONE"] += 1
1040 |                 if first_visible_row < 0:
1041 |                     first_visible_row = row
1042 |                 if int(id_item.data(Qt.ItemDataRole.UserRole)) == selected_id:
1043 |                     target_row = row
1044 | 
1045 |         if target_row >= 0:
1046 |             self.table.selectRow(target_row)
1047 |         elif first_visible_row >= 0:
1048 |             self.table.selectRow(first_visible_row)
1049 |         else:
1050 |             self.table.clearSelection()
1051 | 
1052 |         total_count = len(self._all_remnants)
1053 |         prefix = (
1054 |             f"Mostrando {visible_count} de {total_count} retazos"
1055 |             if (query or fp_code != "ALL")
1056 |             else f"{total_count} retazo{'s' if total_count != 1 else ''} en inventario"
1057 |         )
1058 |         breakdown_str = (
1059 |             f"FP45: {fp_breakdown['FP45']}  |  "
1060 |             f"FP67: {fp_breakdown['FP67']}  |  "
1061 |             f"FP112: {fp_breakdown['FP112']}"
1062 |         )
1063 |         if fp_breakdown["NONE"]:
1064 |             breakdown_str += f"  |  Sin FP: {fp_breakdown['NONE']}"
1065 |         self.summary_label.setText(
1066 |             f"{prefix}   ·   Superficie: {visible_area_cm2:,.1f} cm²   ({breakdown_str})"
1067 |         )
1068 |         self._actualizar_estado_botones()
1069 | 
1070 |     def _agregar(self):
1071 |         available_materials = self.inventory.materiales()
1072 |         if not available_materials:
1073 |             QMessageBox.information(
1074 |                 self,
1075 |                 "Catálogo vacío",
1076 |                 "Da de alta al menos un material antes de añadir retazos.",
1077 |             )
1078 |             self._administrar_materiales()
1079 |             available_materials = self.inventory.materiales()
1080 |             if not available_materials:
1081 |                 return
1082 |         dialog = _DialogoDatosRetazo(
1083 |             available_materials,
1084 |             parent=self,
1085 |             inventory=self.inventory,
1086 |             allow_batch=True,
1087 |         )
1088 |         if not dialog.exec():
1089 |             return
1090 |         try:
1091 |             count = dialog.batch_count()
1092 |             if count > 1 and hasattr(self.inventory, "agregar_lote"):
1093 |                 ids = self.inventory.agregar_lote(
1094 |                     **dialog.values(), cantidad=count
1095 |                 )
1096 |                 remnant_id = ids[-1]
1097 |                 self._refresh(remnant_id)
1098 |                 self._mostrar_aviso_temporal(
1099 |                     f"Registrados {len(ids)} retazos: #{ids[0]} a #{ids[-1]}"
1100 |                 )
1101 |             else:
1102 |                 remnant_id = self.inventory.agregar(**dialog.values())
1103 |                 self._refresh(remnant_id)
1104 |                 self._mostrar_aviso_temporal(f"Guardado como retazo #{remnant_id}")
1105 |         except (ValueError, OSError) as error:
1106 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Retazo no guardado", str(error))
1107 | 
1108 |     def _editar(self):
1109 |         remnant_id = self._selected_id()
1110 |         if remnant_id is None:
1111 |             return
1112 |         remnant = next(
1113 |             (item for item in self.inventory.listar() if item["id"] == remnant_id),
1114 |             None,
1115 |         )
1116 |         if remnant is None:
1117 |             return
1118 |         dialog = _DialogoDatosRetazo(
1119 |             self.inventory.materiales(),
1120 |             remnant,
1121 |             self,
1122 |             inventory=self.inventory,
1123 |             allow_batch=False,
1124 |         )
1125 |         if not dialog.exec():
1126 |             return
1127 |         try:
1128 |             self.inventory.actualizar(remnant_id, **dialog.values())
1129 |         except (ValueError, OSError, KeyError) as error:
1130 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Retazo no actualizado", str(error))
1131 |             return
1132 |         self._refresh(remnant_id)
1133 |         self._mostrar_aviso_temporal(f"Retazo #{remnant_id} actualizado")
1134 | 
1135 |     def _duplicar(self):
1136 |         remnant_id = self._selected_id()
1137 |         if remnant_id is None:
1138 |             return
1139 |         remnant = next(
1140 |             (item for item in self.inventory.listar() if item["id"] == remnant_id),
1141 |             None,
1142 |         )
1143 |         if remnant is None:
1144 |             return
1145 |         dialog = _DialogoDatosRetazo(
1146 |             self.inventory.materiales(),
1147 |             remnant,
1148 |             self,
1149 |             inventory=self.inventory,
1150 |             allow_batch=True,
1151 |             is_duplicate=True,
1152 |         )
1153 |         if not dialog.exec():
1154 |             return
1155 |         try:
1156 |             count = dialog.batch_count()
1157 |             if count > 1 and hasattr(self.inventory, "agregar_lote"):
1158 |                 ids = self.inventory.agregar_lote(
1159 |                     **dialog.values(), cantidad=count
1160 |                 )
1161 |                 new_id = ids[-1]
1162 |                 self._refresh(new_id)
1163 |                 self._mostrar_aviso_temporal(
1164 |                     f"Duplicados {len(ids)} retazos: #{ids[0]} a #{ids[-1]}"
1165 |                 )
1166 |             else:
1167 |                 new_id = self.inventory.agregar(**dialog.values())
1168 |                 self._refresh(new_id)
1169 |                 self._mostrar_aviso_temporal(f"Duplicado como retazo #{new_id}")
1170 |         except (ValueError, OSError) as error:
1171 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Retazo no duplicado", str(error))
1172 | 
1173 |     def _eliminar(self):
1174 |         remnant_id = self._selected_id()
1175 |         if remnant_id is None:
1176 |             return
1177 |         try:
1178 |             action = elegir_accion_retazo_usado(self.inventory, remnant_id, self)
1179 |         except (KeyError, OSError, ValueError) as error:
1180 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Retazo no actualizado", str(error))
1181 |             return
1182 |         if action is None:
1183 |             return
1184 |         if action == "deleted":
1185 |             self._refresh()
1186 |             self._mostrar_aviso_temporal(f"Retazo #{remnant_id} eliminado")
1187 |         else:
1188 |             self._refresh(remnant_id)
1189 |             self._mostrar_aviso_temporal(
1190 |                 f"Retazo #{remnant_id} actualizado con sobrante"
1191 |             )

1193 |     def _importar_csv(self):
1194 |         filename, _ = QFileDialog.getOpenFileName(
1195 |             self,
1196 |             "Importar retazos",
1197 |             "",
1198 |             "Archivos CSV (*.csv)",
1199 |         )
1200 |         if not filename:
1201 |             return
1202 |         try:
1203 |             imported = self.inventory.importar_csv(filename)
1204 |         except (OSError, ValueError) as error:
1205 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "CSV no importado", str(error))
1206 |             return
1207 |         self._refresh()
1208 |         self._mostrar_aviso_temporal(f"{imported} retazos importados")
1209 |         QMessageBox.information(
1210 |             self,
1211 |             "Importación terminada",
1212 |             f"Se añadieron {imported} retazos al inventario.",
1213 |         )

1218 |             self,

1265 | 
1266 | class DialogoAcomodosRetazos(QDialog):
1267 |     def __init__(self, results, inventory, parent=None, summary=None):
1268 |         super().__init__(parent)

1390 |                 layout_result=self.selected_result,
1391 |             )
1392 |         except (KeyError, OSError, ValueError) as error:
1393 |             QMessageBox.warning(self, "Retazo no actualizado", str(error))
1394 |             return
1395 |         removed_row = self.table.currentRow()
1396 |         if action is None:
1397 |             return
1398 |         rows_to_remove = [
1399 |             index
1400 |             for index, result in enumerate(self.results)
1401 |             if result["retazo_id"] == remnant_id
1402 |         ]
1403 |         for index in reversed(rows_to_remove):
1404 |             self.table.removeRow(index)
1405 |         self.results = [
1406 |             result for result in self.results if result["retazo_id"] != remnant_id
1407 |         ]
1408 |         self.selected_result = None
1409 |         self.canvas.actualizar_grafico(None)
1410 |         self.use_button.setEnabled(False)
1411 |         self.remove_button.setEnabled(False)
1412 |         if not self.results:
1413 |             self.reject()
1414 |         else:
1415 |             next_row = min(max(removed_row, 0), len(self.results) - 1)
1416 |             self.table.selectRow(next_row)
1417 |             self._show_selected(next_row, 0, -1, 0)
